### [1] [Spin Hamiltonian for Quantum Simulation of Minimally Truncated $3+1$D SU(2) Lattice Gauge Theory and Testing Eigenstate Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36608)
*Ziyuan Li,Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了在最小截断 j_max=1/2 下的 3+1 维纯 SU(2) 格点规范理论的空间规范单态哈密顿量，并将其映射到量子比特上；通过精确对角化 2×3×3 格点系统，验证了本征态热化假说（ETH），确认其在耦合强度 g=0.3 处呈现量子遍历性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在数字量子处理器上模拟非平衡动力学提供可实现的、满足规范不变性的低维量子模拟平台，并检验高维规范理论是否满足本征态热化假说（ETH）。

Method: 在最小自旋截断 j_max = 1/2 下，利用高斯定律约束与规范不变环算符的冗余性，提取最小独立环变量集，建立到量子比特的一一映射；将哈密顿量表示为 Pauli 字符串之和；对 2×3×3 格点（固定边界）进行精确对角化，分析能级统计、局域能量密度矩阵元及本征态纠缠熵。

Result: 2×3×3 格点需 28 个量子比特；在 g=0.3 处观测到 Wigner-Dyson 能级分布、ETH 相容的矩阵元衰减行为及体积律纠缠熵；证实 3+1D 截断 SU(2) 规范理论满足 ETH。

Conclusion: 3+1 维截断 SU(2) 格点规范理论在量子模拟可及规模下满足本征态热化假说，为基于 NISQ 设备的实时非平衡规范场模拟提供了首个严格基准。

Abstract: We construct the spatial-gauge-singlet Hamiltonian of pure SU(2) lattice gauge theory in $3+1$ dimensions ($3+1$D) on qubits under the minimal truncation $j_{\rm max}=1/2$, building on previous work on the hyperhoneycomb lattice, and test the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). By enforcing Gauss's law and exploiting the redundancy among gauge-invariant loop operators, we find the minimal set of independent loop variables that can be bijectively mapped onto qubits, and write the Hamiltonian as a sum of Pauli strings. An $n\times n\times n$ lattice with fixed boundary conditions requires $2(n-1)^3+3(n-1)^2$ qubits. We exactly diagonalize the Hamiltonian on a $2\times 3\times 3$ lattice and identify the coupling range of quantum ergodicity from the level statistics. At the ergodic coupling $g=0.3$, we calculate the matrix elements of local energy density operators and the entanglement entropies of all eigenstates and find them consistent with the ETH. These results show that the $3+1$D truncated SU(2) lattice gauge theory thermalizes in accordance with the ETH and provide a benchmark for future real-time quantum simulations of non-equilibrium dynamics.

</details>


### [2] [Easier parameter scans with hmcdj](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37120)
*Alexis Verney-Provatas,Ed Bennett,Gaurav Ray*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: hmcdj 是一个基于 Grid 库开发的开源 C++ 工具，用于高效生成大规模格点规范理论（如 QCD）的蒙特卡洛系综，支持 YAML 配置、自动调参（如 MD 步长）、ILDG 格式输出及 HPC 调度器集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化大规模格点规范理论蒙特卡洛系综生成流程，降低用户在理论建模、参数控制与高性能计算调度方面的工程负担。

Method: 基于 Grid 库构建，采用 C++ 实现可扩展的格点理论定义框架；通过 YAML 文件实现运行时参数化配置；集成自适应分子动力学步长调整、ILDG 标准化 I/O 与 Slurm/PBS 等 HPC 调度器接口。

Result: 实现了高灵活性、低样板代码的系综生成工具 hmcdj，支持自动化调参、标准化数据输出与动态轨迹计数适配，已在实际格点 QCD 计算中部署验证。

Conclusion: hmcdj 显著提升了格点规范理论数值模拟的可重复性、可扩展性与 HPC 资源利用率，为大尺度第一性原理强子物理计算提供了可靠基础设施。

Abstract: We present hmcdj, a new tool developed to ease the process of generating large numbers of gauge ensembles for a given theory. Building on top of the Grid library, hmcdj allows quick specification of lattice gauge theories in C++ with minimal boilerplate, and precise specification of per-ensemble parameters to be controlled at run time from a YAML input file. Additionally, it supports many quality-of-life features for managing large numbers of running jobs, including automatic tuning of the molecular dynamics step size to control the Metropolis acceptance rate; output of ILDG-compliant data; and integration with HPC schedulers to dynamically adapt the target trajectory count of a job to match its time limits. hmcdj is openly available under the GPLv2 license, and welcomes new contributions.

</details>

### [3] [Analytic Bootstrap Solution for Quantum Mechanics on a Circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36031)
*David Berenstein,Upamanyu Moitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子力学自举法求解圆周上粒子薛定谔方程的方法，通过傅里叶系数递归关系的指数衰减解导出广义连分式表示，并可解析计算态密度、波函数与准动量，成功应用于Mathieu方程并解析分辨指数小能带。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展一种不依赖传统微分方程数值求解、而基于物理约束（如傅里叶系数指数衰减）的解析/半解析方法，以高精度处理周期势下的谱问题（如能带结构）。

Method: 量子力学自举法：将薛定谔方程转化为关于测度傅里叶系数的线性递归关系；施加物理可接受性条件——解必须指数衰减；由此导出能量本征值满足的广义连分式方程；利用该结构解析延拓能量-耦合参数空间，直接计算态密度ρ(E)、波函数及准动量k(E)。

Result: 获得能量本征值E作为耦合常数和准动量k的解析函数；态密度ρ(E)由单变量不等式判据验证物理性；波函数可通过逆傅里叶变换重构；准动量k(E)由边界条件或布洛赫相位确定；在Mathieu方程中精确复现指数小能隙（band width ∼ e^{-c/√ε}）。

Conclusion: 量子自举法为一维周期势谱问题提供了严格、解析且高精度的替代框架，其核心是将物理可接受性（指数衰减）编码为递归解的存在性条件，进而导出连分式特征方程，适用于强耦合与窄带极限。

Abstract: We solve the Schrödinger equation for a particle in the circle with a quantum mechanical bootstrap method. The main idea is that in physical situations one needs solutions of the recursion relations for the Fourier coefficients of the measure that vanish exponentially fast. We show that this results in a (generalized) continued fraction representation of the solution. The solution is an analytical function of the couplings and the energy. We also show how one this method directly computes the density of states and checking that the answer is physical is reduced to checking an inequality for a single variable. We also show how to reconstruct the wave function and how to extract the quasimomentum of the particle as a function of the energy. We illustrate our solution in the simplest case of the Mathieu equation, where we show that the method can easily resolve exponentially small bands in the band spectrum.

</details>


### [4] [The Disordered Universe 2025: Analytic Properties of Amplitudes and Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36271)
*Mattia Capuano,Chandramouli Chowdhury,Chantelle Esper,Alessandro Palazio,Jonah Stalknecht,Yong Sheng Yap,Andrew J. McLeod,Paolo Benincasa,Guilherme Leite Pimentel*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了研究微扰量子场论中圈图散射振幅和关联函数解析性质的现代技术，重点包括Landau分析、幺正性与因果性对费曼积分解析结构的约束，以及利用迭代积分符号演算和bootstrap方法从解析结构确定费曼积分函数形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微扰量子场论中圈图振幅与关联函数的解析结构是揭示其物理内涵（如奇点结构、幺正性、因果性）与数学结构（如多重对数、霍奇结构）之间深层联系的关键需求。

Method: 基于Landau方程的奇点分析；结合幺正割线条件与因果性要求推导费曼积分的解析性约束；运用迭代积分（iterated integrals）及其symbol calculus构建解析函数空间；采用bootstrap策略，仅凭奇点位置、分支切割与单值性等解析数据重构积分结果。

Result: 系统建立了从Landau奇点出发，经幺正/因果性约束，到symbol bootstrap求解费曼积分的完整逻辑链；展示了如何将物理原理直接编码为函数空间的代数约束，并成功应用于多腿、多尺度费曼积分的解析构造。

Conclusion: 费曼积分的解析结构并非偶然，而是由基本量子场论原理（幺正性、因果性、局域性）严格决定；symbol bootstrap方法提供了一种不依赖具体积分计算、仅凭解析性公理即可高效率确定高阶微扰结果的范式。

Abstract: These lectures provide an introduction to modern techniques for studying the analytic properties of loop-level scattering amplitudes and correlation functions in perturbative quantum field theory. We begin by developing the ideas behind Landau analysis, which identifies the location and nature of kinematic singularities in Feynman integrals (the universal building blocks that contribute to perturbative amplitudes and correlators). We then show how fundamental principles such as unitarity and causality constrain the analytic structure of Feynman integrals, and develop methods for working out some of these constraints in practice. Finally, we connect these observations to the properties of iterated integrals and their symbol calculus, to show how bootstrap methods can be used to determine the functional form of Feynman integrals from just knowledge of their analytic structure. These notes are based on a series of lectures held during the La Ricotta Summer School "The Disordered Universe 2025" from 21~July to 1~August~2025.

</details>

*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于Nambu未发表笔记发展量子流体动力学理论，引入配置空间度规与机械联络，导出可观测量形变的量子信道；证明动量对间相干性精确保持的判据，揭示联络曲率对去相干的不可消除作用；对两类剪切流找到精确无噪符号子系统，并展示通过调控生成流幅值可显著抑制初始去相干，但该优势在条件演化中逐渐消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个能统一描述流体宏观经典行为与微观量子退相干效应的理论框架，特别是从几何结构（如度规与联络）出发理解量子-经典过渡中的信息丢失机制。

Method: 基于Nambu未发表笔记建立量子流体动力学模型；定义动能作用量诱导的配置空间黎曼度规与机械联络；将未观测流体自由度视为环境并对其求迹，得到可观测形变的量子信道；分析联络曲率对动量空间相干性的约束；构造特定剪切流解以识别噪声子空间与Euler残差抵消条件。

Result: 1) 给出动量对间量子相干性精确保持的几何判据；2) 证明非零联络曲率导致该相干耦合不可规范去除；3) 在两类剪切流中发现精确无噪符号子系统；4) 展示通过适配主导耦合流的振幅可最小化初始二次去相干率；5) 发现初始低衰减率的制备态可能在有限读出时间内经历更大的最大采样损失。

Conclusion: 流体系统的量子退相干本质上由其配置空间的几何结构（度规与联络曲率）决定；经典极限并非单纯由尺度分离主导，而是受控于可观测量与隐变量之间的几何耦合；最优量子制备需权衡初始衰减率与时间演化鲁棒性。

Abstract: We develop a quantum hydrodynamic theory based on Nambu's unpublished note \emph{A New Look at Fluid Dynamics}. The kinetic action defines a metric on configuration space and a mechanical connection coupling an observable cyclic deformation to unobserved fluid modes. Tracing out these modes yields a quantum channel for the observed deformation. We establish a criterion for the exact preservation of coherence between opposite momenta and show how nonzero connection curvature prevents this coupling from being removed. The leading quadratic decay of quantum coherence is determined by the prepared quantum state. For two shear flows, we identify an exact noiseless sign subsystem. Introducing additional generating flows cancels the initial residual of the Euler equations while producing nonzero connection curvature, governed by the same kinetic integrals that determine the classical correction. At fixed mean energy, choosing amplitudes adapted to the dominant coupling flow substantially reduces the initial decoherence, even along the direction that cancels the Euler residual. This advantage gradually diminishes under conditional evolution, eventually leading to a crossing of the decoherence curves. Our example shows that a preparation with a smaller initial coherence-decay coefficient can have a larger maximum sampled loss over a finite readout interval.

</details>


### [6] [MPS overlaps and Sklyanin determinants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37285)
*Tamas Gombor*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了正交与辛扭杨代数（twisted Yangians）的有限维不可约评价表示所对应的可积矩阵乘积态（MPS）的显式重叠函数；核心是将普适重叠公式中的交换拟行列式关联到嵌套子代数的Sklyanin行列式，并在正交情形中直接给出Gelfand–Tsetlin基下的谱，在辛情形中结合Sklyanin行列式、子式及边界矩阵分解得到谱；最终重叠函数由Gelfand–Tsetlin图案参数化，仅依赖于表示的最高权数据，并与Gaudin行列式比值及伪真空重叠系数共同决定归一化MPS重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可积开放自旋链中矩阵乘积态（MPS）与本征态之间的精确重叠，尤其针对正交和辛对称性下扭杨代数描述的系统，需克服嵌套Bethe ansatz中边界条件与高秩代数结构带来的技术难点。

Method: 利用扭杨代数的代数Bethe ansatz框架，将普适重叠公式中的交换quasi-determinants通过递归关系映射为嵌套子代数的Sklyanin行列式；正交情形中借助Gelfand–Tsetlin基实现谱的显式解析，辛情形中引入边界矩阵的rank-two因子分解并耦合Sklyanin子式；所有构造保持最高权表示结构不变。

Result: 获得正交/辛扭杨代数任意有限维不可约评价表示下可积MPS的显式重叠函数，形式为Gelfand–Tsetlin图案标记的乘积表达式，仅依赖最高权；同时将正交情形推广至非评价表示，以嵌套扭杨代数子代数的最高权参数化重叠。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了扭正交/辛可积系统中MPS与Bethe态间重叠的统一代数构造，将重叠问题完全还原为表示论数据（最高权与Gelfand–Tsetlin图案），为计算边界熵、量子淬火可观测量及对称保护拓扑序提供了严格工具。

Abstract: We derive explicit overlap functions for integrable matrix product states associated with arbitrary finite-dimensional irreducible evaluation representations of the orthogonal and symplectic twisted Yangians. The central step is to relate the commuting quasi-determinants entering the universal overlap formula to Sklyanin determinants of nested subalgebras. For orthogonal evaluation representations, this relation determines their spectra directly in the Gelfand--Tsetlin basis. In the symplectic case, we combine Sklyanin determinants and minors with a factorization of the nested rank-two boundary matrices to obtain the corresponding spectra. The resulting overlap functions are explicit products parametrized by Gelfand--Tsetlin patterns and require only the highest-weight data of the underlying orthogonal or symplectic representation. Together with the universal ratio of Gaudin determinants and the model-dependent pseudovacuum overlap coefficients, they determine the normalized MPS overlaps. We also express the overlap functions in the orthogonal case beyond evaluation representations in terms of the highest weights arising along the nested twisted-Yangian subalgebras.

</details>

Method: 采用经典约束Hamilton力学框架，构造反射相空间，分析约束的可微性、弱一阶性及哈密顿流对边界域的保持性；计算诱导边界代数；比较正则嵌入自由度；显式构造四维混合扇区解族；分析特征/非特征端点的动力学行为；推导位移Dirichlet条件下的典范仿射提升与动量修正。

Conclusion: Carroll–Weyl零弦在反射相空间中展现出丰富而自洽的边界几何与代数结构，其约束动力学、辛结构与对称性延拓均严格适配Carrollian极限下的因果结构与边界条件。

Abstract: We construct a classical reflected phase space for open Carroll--Weyl null strings with Dirichlet conditions at the target origin or linear mixed target gluings. The reflection-compatible constraints are differentiable, their Hamiltonian flows preserve the boundary domain, and the constraint system is weakly first class on its regular part. The induced boundary algebra extends the boundary Carrollian conformal algebra by an even weight-one Carroll--Weyl current, with normalization fixed by the reflected symplectic form. The regular local embedding count agrees with that of the closed theory. We exhibit a four-dimensional mixed sector with one canonical field pair and a separate solution family carrying a nonzero conserved target-space boost charge and a nondegenerate symplectic pullback. Smooth fixed-point reflection selects characteristic endpoints. For timelike defects with natural tangential variations and no additional boundary interactions, non-characteristic endpoints have vanishing Carroll flow. For displaced Dirichlet data, we derive the canonical affine lift and its required shift of the edge momentum by integrated bulk momentum.

</details>


### [8] [The Art of Orbifolding by Huge Non-Abelian Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36024)
*Jan Albert,Masaki Okada,Yuji Tachikawa*

TL;DR: 本文计算了由Conway群等巨大非阿贝尔群作用于二维手性费米子所得到的orbifold的环面配分函数，揭示并解决了该问题在原理与实践层面长期未决的多个关键难题；其技术动机源于寻找拓扑模形式（tmf）的周期性元素，但最终所考虑的orbifold并未实现该目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找拓扑模形式（topological modular forms, tmf）的周期性元素（periodicity element），该元素需满足特定上同调与模不变性条件，而手性费米子orbifold的环面配分函数可编码tmf谱序列中的关键微分结构。

Method: 发展了一套适用于大非阿贝尔群（特别是Conway群Co0及其子群）作用下chiral fermion orbifold的系统性轨道求和（orbit sum）、投影算符构造、相因子一致性检验及模不变性提升方法；特别处理了sporadic group表示论中无标准特征标表、共轭类判定困难、以及自旋结构与射影表示兼容性等核心障碍。

Result: 首次完整计算出若干Conway群相关orbifold的torus partition function，严格验证其SL(2,Z)模不变性，并确认其对应谱序列中d3微分的非平凡性；同时证伪了这些orbifold作为tmf周期性元素的可能性。

Abstract: We compute torus partition functions of orbifolds of two-dimensional chiral fermions by huge non-Abelian groups involving the Conway group, one of the sporadic finite simple groups. This seemingly standard problem turns out to contain, somewhat unexpectedly, multiple major issues hitherto unresolved, both at the level of the principle and at the level of practice. We describe in detail how they can be overcome. Although the methods described in this paper are generally applicable, the choice of the particular orbifolds we consider in this paper was motivated by the search for the periodicity element of topological modular forms. We will provide a brief introduction to this question, to explain why all the techniques in this paper had to be developed. Unfortunately, the orbifolds we considered turned out not to be the periodicity element.

</details>


### [9] [Instanton Structure from 325-Loop 3D $ϕ^3$ Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36025)

Abstract: In theories with compact extra dimensions, the four-dimensional effective description is obtained by Kaluza-Klein reduction. The state available to a four-dimensional observer is the reduced density matrix obtained by tracing the higher-dimensional vacuum over the tower of massive modes, and it need not be pure. In this paper we study the momentum-space entanglement of this state for a five-dimensional scalar on $M_4\times S^1$ with a cubic or quartic self-interaction. We compute, at leading order in the coupling, the mutual information $I_n$ between the zero modes below a resolution scale $\mu$ and a single Kaluza-Klein level $n$. When the zero modes couple linearly and without derivatives to pairs of tower modes, $I_n$ falls as $1/m_n$, where $m_n$ is the mass of level $n$. In the cubic theory, this tail is independent of the ultraviolet cutoff, with coefficient $(\mu R)^2/1024\pi^3$ for compactification radius $R$, and the mutual information with the whole tower grows logarithmically with the number of levels. In the quartic theory, the tail is masked by states containing additional hard quanta, which make $I_n$ depend on the cutoff. Compared with the corrections the tower makes to low-energy amplitudes, which are dominated by the lightest levels and captured by local operators, the mutual information with the tower is not dominated by any finite set of levels and arises from the time-nonlocal part of the effective action. The local four-dimensional effective theory therefore describes the low-energy observables of the reduced system but not its state. Unbroken supersymmetry cancels the vacuum energy of the compactification but not the mutual information, which at leading order is a sum of squared amplitudes.

While the lecture notes related to each course taught at the school will be published separately and are inevitably devoted to both conceptual and technical aspects of each of these topics, the school was not meant to be a collage of independent topics: the present paper has the purpose of outlining the global vision, how cosmology ends up being a unifier and benefits from both their viewpoint and techniques, how all the concepts and techniques originally coming from these four different areas are part of the same cosmological narrative, why this point of view deserves to be explored, and drawing perspectives for the future. Abstract: We study how magnetic fields affect the onset of quark saturation, which limits the domain of applicability of a purely baryonic description. We consider matter composed of electrically neutral $ddu$ baryons, including neutron- and $\Delta^0$-like states. Using a simple constituent-quark model, we compute the baryon wave functions and spectra in magnetic fields. The magnetic field polarizes the spin-flavor structure, compresses the internal wave function in the transverse direction, and strongly suppresses the transverse kinetic energy. From the baryon wave functions, we construct the one-body reduced density matrix and derive the corresponding single-quark distributions in longitudinal momentum and Landau-level space. We then use these distributions to evaluate quark occupation probabilities in an ideal baryon gas and determine the onset of quark saturation. At finite magnetic field in the $+z$-direction, the $d_\downarrow$ quark in the lowest Landau level reaches saturation first. The resulting saturation density depends nonmonotonically on the magnetic-field strength: it decreases at weak fields and increases at stronger fields. This behavior reflects the competition between spin-flavor polarization, which promotes saturation, and transverse spatial squeezing, which delays saturation by broadening of the quark transverse momentum distribution.

The clock input is $\dot\alpha/\alpha=1.0(1.1)\times10^{-18}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$. It matches the WEP marginal reach when $|\zeta|=2.37\times10^{-8}\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, with $\zeta=\dot\varphi/\alpha_E$. For the benchmark $\dot\varphi=\xi H_0$ and $\alpha_E=10^{-3}\alpha_0$, this gives $\xi/\alpha_0\simeq0.34$. A model becomes testable after it supplies $r=D_{\hat m}/D_e$, the scalar amplitude, and $\zeta$. The Damour--Piazza--Veneziano runaway-dilaton estimate lies near $\eta\sim10^{-12}$ for order-unity $n=2$ parameters, well above the MICROSCOPE-level WEP scale. In the representative-isotope two-charge model, a MICROSCOPE-level Be--Ti-like row would reduce the long WEP axis by a factor $27.8$; a repeated Ti--Pt-like row would barely change it. The calculation tests compactification-level predictions for string-derived dilaton or modulus sectors after the model supplies its low-energy data.

Abstract: We investigate the relativistic kinematics, spinor-recoil structure, and direct-detection phenomenology of a Dirac dark matter fermion interacting with SM charged leptons through a real scalar mediator within a low-energy effective Lagrangian in the fermion mass basis. Standard halo direct-detection searches lose sensitivity for sub-MeV dark matter, because the maximum recoil energy falls below detector thresholds. When halo dark matter particles are upscattered to relativistic energies by galactic cosmic-ray electrons (CRE), the momentum transfer in both the astrophysical upscattering stage and the terrestrial detection stage can exceed the fermion mass scales. By decomposing the tree-level $t$-channel cross section into three positive-definite contributions, we separate the static-numerator contribution from the linear and quadratic momentum-transfer spinor-recoil contributions, evaluated via convergent power series that reduce subtractive cancellation at small momentum transfer. Furthermore, retaining the dynamical scalar propagator yields a high-energy cross section that decreases as $1/s$ and satisfies $s$-wave helicity partial-wave unitarity, in contrast to the linear $\mathcal{O}(s/m_S^4)$ growth of the contact approximation. Applying the exact differential recoil spectra within an effective propagation model for cosmic-ray electron boosted dark matter (CRE-BDM), we derive $90\%$ C.L. exclusion bounds in the 1 keV -- 10 MeV mass range from Super-Kamiokande, XENONnT, and PandaX-4T, extending experimental sensitivity into the sub-MeV window.

At leading order, these kernels reduce to: (i) DM bubble diagrams in the case of four-point interactions, and (ii) dressed in-medium mediator propagators in the case of long-range interactions, where the dressing is provided by the DM environment. The ALP miracle scenario prefers the ALP mass above about $10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}$, far larger than other popular ALP dark matter models. Abstract: Machine learning tasks in particle physics are typically expressed in terms of regression, or relaxation toward some optimal solution as defined by an objective or loss function. For concreteness, we study regression with energy flow polynomials (EFPs), a complete linear basis of infrared and collinear safe observables on Lorentz invariant phase space. While the expectation value of any EFP scales like a constant in the large multiplicity limit, we prove that the covariance on uniform phase space of any two EFPs is highly suppressed and scales like the inverse of the squared multiplicity. This very unintuitive result relies on detailed cancellation guaranteed by the interplay of permutation invariance, momentum conservation, and rotational invariance. This scaling of the covariance with multiplicity further implies that the coefficients in an expansion of EFPs must scale quadratically in multiplicity. We present an example of regression with EFPs as applied to $e^+e^-\to$ hadrons events, and show how the regression can be solved analytically through the complementary expansions of the large multiplicity limit and of familiar fixed-order perturbation theory of QCD.

Abstract: Forward-backward multiplicity correlations probe the longitudinal structure of particle production, but fluctuations in the number of emitting sources complicate their interpretation. Every observable is normalized to unity for statistically independent particles, vanishes when both window multiplicities are fixed, and remains finite at equal positive window means. We give explicit expressions through fourth order and an all-order recursion. Fits constrained by the mean multiplicities and second-order observables provide conditional higher-order benchmarks. Deviations of these benchmarks from the PYTHIA results test the assumed correlation hierarchy, subject to the quality of the lower-order fit. Preliminary ALICE measurements at $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV are used to illustrate the corresponding model-dependent scenarios.

### [75] [What the Primary Lund Plane Omits: A Model-Stitching Diagnosis of a Jet-Tagger Representation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37137)
*Saurabh Rai,Sanmay Ganguly*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过模型拼接（model stitching）技术，区分Lund-plane与Particle Transformer（ParT）在喷注标记任务中性能差异的根源：是表征能力不足（representational deficit），还是下游计算能力不足（computational deficit）。结果表明，Lund-plane（尤其仅用主退簇支）的缺陷本质上是表征性的；补全Cambridge/Aachen完整退簇树可修复大部分缺陷，且关键信息集中在主支上的大角度分裂（如top/W开角附近），而非高阶关联或次级分裂本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分Jet tagging中不同表征（如Lund-plane vs. ParT）性能差异的根源——是输入表征本身信息不足，还是下游网络架构/学习能力受限。传统独立评估无法解耦二者，需因果性干预手段。

Method: 采用model stitching：将Lund表征网络的冻结早期层与ParT的冻结晚期层之间插入一个可训练的线性映射；对比不同Lund变体（主支、分裂三元组超图、完整C/A树）拼接到同一ParT下游时的背景抑制能力；辅以节点掩蔽（masking）、AUC与低信号效率下背景拒绝率分析、以及局域化缺陷喷注统计。

Result: 1) 主支Lund拼接至ParT下游后，背景拒绝率显著低于ParT自身，证明表征缺陷主导；2) 超图读取分裂三元组未改善性能，排除高阶关联缺失；3) 缺陷集中于0.6%高质量背景喷注（m≥mtop，多粒子少主分裂）；4) 使用完整C/A树后，独立Lund性能提升，填补72% top-tagging拒绝率差距；5) 掩蔽实验证明次级分裂不直接提供判别信息，而是使表征更易学习；6) 主支中宽角分裂（near top/W opening angles）的信息价值≈2×随机分裂；7) q/g tagging中主支缺陷较小（保留77.6% ParT拒绝率），完整树仅补33%缺口。

Conclusion: Lund-plane在top tagging中的性能瓶颈主要源于其标准主支表征对特定高质背景喷注的信息覆盖不足，而非下游网络能力；完整C/A树通过增强表征可学习性（而非注入新判别特征）显著缓解该缺陷；关键物理信息位于主支的大角度分裂结构，支持基于物理启发的表征设计优化。

Abstract: Lund-plane and Particle Transformer (ParT) jet taggers can differ in performance because one representation is weaker, or because a weaker downstream computation reads an adequate one. Standalone performance cannot separate these. Model stitching can: it links the frozen early layers of one network to the frozen late layers of another through a single trained linear map. As a positive control we use an incomplete Lund representation, the primary declustering branch, whose deficit is known independently. Stitched into ParT's downstream, it reaches a background rejection at least as much as its own downstream recovers, yet far below ParT's background rejection: the deficit is representational, not computational. A hypergraph reader over splitting triplets does no better, ruling out higher-order correlations as the cause. The gap is invisible in AUC, which stays within 1\% of its target value, and appears only in background rejection at low signal efficiency. It localizes to 0.6\% of background jets, at and above the top mass, with more constituents but fewer primary splittings than typical background. Rebuilding the representation on the full Cambridge/Aachen declustering tree repairs the deficit as predicted: standalone rejection rises, closing 72\% of the gap. Masking nodes at inference shows the repaired network still relies almost entirely on the primary branch; the secondary splittings make the representation learnable rather than supplying information it reads. Along the primary branch, the widest-angle splitting, near the top and $W$ opening angles, is worth nearly twice a random one. For quark/gluon tagging the primary branch is far less deficient, retaining 77.6\% of ParT's rejection (46.9\% for top), and the full tree closes only 33\% of the gap (72\% for top).

</details>

Abstract: Global analyses of nuclear parton distribution functions (nPDFs) have traditionally used the Hessian method for uncertainty estimation, relying on a local quadratic approximation of the chi-squared function. This Gaussian approximation can be inadequate when experimental constraints are limited and parameter correlations are strong, as it cannot fully capture asymmetric uncertainties, flat directions, or multiple minima. This thesis develops a Bayesian framework for nPDF determination using Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) techniques within the nCTEQ analysis framework. An adaptive Metropolis-Hastings algorithm enables direct sampling of the posterior distribution, revealing pronounced non-Gaussian behavior and secondary minima, particularly in the valence distributions. The analysis presents the first global single-nucleus determination of lead (Pb) PDFs based exclusively on lead data, complemented by a multi-nuclei analysis incorporating different nuclear targets through a standard analytic dependence on the mass number. Including lighter nuclei significantly constrains quark distributions and changes the extracted lead PDFs, while the gluon distribution remains comparatively stable. A comparison with Hessian-based results highlights the limitations of the Gaussian approximation in regions with strong nonlinearity or multimodality. Overall, this work establishes Bayesian MCMC inference as a flexible framework for nPDF determination, providing a more comprehensive characterization of parameter uncertainties and the underlying parameter space.

Abstract: In light QCD axion models, finite-density corrections to the axion potential can source a nonvanishing axion field inside neutron stars.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在QCD领阶α_s^5下，采用完整五味夸克方案（full 5FNS），数值计算pp→Φjjj的全微分散射截面；严格包含顶夸克与底夸克圈图的相干干涉；覆盖全部部分子过程（gg, gq, qq̄等）；采用精确的夸克质量依赖振幅（非HEFT或FF-HEFT近似）；构造喷注可观测量：p_T^Φ、m_jj、z*、Δy_jj、Φ₂，并定义CP敏感不对称量A_Φ₂ ∝ sin2Φ₂。

Conclusion: pp→Φjjj是探测Higgs CP结构的高灵敏度道；必须采用全质量依赖计算，尤其在高能尾部；Φ₂方位角关联及其sin2Φ₂不对称提供了一种模型无关、无背景污染的CP破坏判据，已在13.6 TeV LHC参数下给出精确预言。

Abstract: A neutral Higgs boson $\Phi$ with general scalar and pseudoscalar couplings and accompanying jets can be efficiently produced via gluon fusion through heavy-quark loops in high-energy hadronic collisions. We present integrated cross sections and kinematic distributions for the production of a generic Higgs boson $\Phi$ in association with three jets, $pp \to \Phi jjj$, at order $\alpha_{s}^{5}$. We include the full mass dependence of the top- and bottom-quark loop contributions, combined coherently so that their interference is retained. Earlier finite-mass calculations of $\Phi+3$ jets with bottom-quark loops and $CP$-odd or $CP$-mixed couplings were restricted to $gg \to ggg\Phi$; we include the complete set of partonic subprocesses, and find that the quark-containing channels supply close to one half of the pseudoscalar three-jet cross section at $\tan\beta=1$. In a Type-II two-Higgs-doublet model the bottom-quark loop comes to dominate the rate as $\tan\beta$ is increased, so that the cross section is non-monotonic in $\tan\beta$. Comparing against the heavy-top effective theory and its form-factor-improved version, we show that the effective description fails in the hard tails of the transverse-momentum and dijet-mass distributions, while the third-jet centrality $z^\star$ and the rapidity separation of the two leading jets are largely insensitive to the heavy-quark treatment. We further study the multi-jet azimuthal correlation $\Phi_{2}$, built from the jet momenta on the two sides of the Higgs boson in rapidity, and find that the bottom-quark contribution modifies its shape even for a state of definite $CP$. For a $CP$-mixed state the interference of the two couplings adds a $\sin2\Phi_2$ term, which a simple counting asymmetry isolates and which no $CP$ eigenstate can produce. Numerical results are presented for the LHC at $\sqrt{s}=13.6~$TeV and $m_\Phi=125.2~$GeV.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理已具备大规模、高保真度的标注模拟数据，这与典型基础模型依赖海量无标签数据形成对比；因此，需探究在标注数据充足前提下，引入自监督目标是否仍有增益。

Method: 提出SuperJEPA：一种联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），额外嵌入监督损失项，实现自监督与监督信号的协同优化；在喷注物理任务上系统评估其在不同下游任务、模型尺寸及预训练数据量下的泛化能力。

Result: SuperJEPA在多个喷注标记任务（如quark/gluon、top/W/Z区分）上显著优于纯监督预训练基线；性能提升在冻结编码器与端到端微调两种范式下均稳健存在；增益随模型规模与预训练数据量扩大而持续。

Conclusion: 在高能物理等标注数据丰富的领域，融合预测型自监督（如JEPA）与监督学习可有效提升表征质量，为构建下一代粒子物理基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models often obtain powerful results through very large unlabeled training datasets that dwarf available labeled datasets in size, but high energy physics already provides large samples of high-fidelity labeled simulation. We therefore ask whether self-supervised objectives improve over supervised pretraining alone when large labeled samples already exist. We introduce SuperJEPA, a joint-embedding predictive architec- ture augmented with a supervised objective, and find improved downstream jet-tagging performance across multiple tasks, model sizes, and pretraining dataset sizes. The gains persist for both frozen and fine-tuned encoders, motivating predictive self-supervision in future large-scale particle-physics foundation models.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在COMPASS实验2022年运行中，采用160 GeV/c高能缪子束轰击横向极化氘核靶，通过单强子半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）过程，对八个横自旋依赖方位角不对称性进行联合拟合提取。

Conclusion: 该成果完成了COMPASS在SIDIS中横自旋物理的全面测量纲领，与已有质子结果共同构成全球TMD全局分析的关键输入，显著提升对核子三维自旋-动量结构的约束能力。

Abstract: Over the years, the COMPASS experiment at CERN has pursued an extensive SIDIS programme aimed at a comprehensive understanding of the structure of the nucleon, using a high-energy muon beam and polarised targets. Within this programme, measurements with a transversely polarised deuteron target were performed in 2022 using the 160 GeV/c muon beam. In single-hadron SIDIS on such a target, eight transverse-spin-dependent azimuthal asymmetries arise, providing sensitivity to several transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution functions and to subleading-twist partonic correlations. We report the simultaneous extraction of all eight asymmetries from the complete 2022 deuteron sample. The Collins and Sivers asymmetries, previously presented using about 70% of the 2022 statistics, are updated with the full sample and found consistent with the earlier result, while the six remaining asymmetries are presented for the first time with the full 2022 statistics. Combined with the existing proton results, these measurements complete a comprehensive COMPASS programme on transverse-spin effects in SIDIS, providing essential input for global analyses.

</details>

*Ying-Hao Wang,Yi-Feng Wu,Pei-Rong Li,Yu Zhang,Xiao-Rui Lyu*

TL;DR: 本文研究了在拟议的超级陶-粲工厂（Super Tau-Charm Facility）上，利用量子关联中性粲介子对（coherent $D\overline{D}$）测量粲夸克混合、间接$C\!P$破坏及强子参数的前景。基于$\sqrt{s}=3.773$和$4.030\,{\rm GeV}$下$e^+e^-\to D^{(*)}\overline{D}^{(*)}$产生的时积双重标记率，结合多种标记模态（味特异、$C\!P$本征态、自共轭末态），在$1\,{\rm ab}^{-1}$每能点积分亮度下评估了参数灵敏度。结果可为CKM角$\gamma$拟合提供独特强子输入，并与Belle II和LHCb的时间依赖测量形成互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定charm mixing参数（$x$, $y$）、间接$C\!P$破坏参数（$|q/p|-1$, $\phi$）、强相位差（$\delta$）和相干因子（$R_f$）对检验标准模型、约束CKM矩阵元（尤其是角$\gamma$）至关重要；现有实验（如Belle II、LHCb）主要依赖时间依赖分析，而量子关联$D\overline{D}$提供无须时间分辨的独立、高精度方法。

Method: 采用时间积分双重标记（double tag）分析：一粒子以味特异衰变（$D\to K^-\pi^+\pi^+\pi^-$, $K^-\pi^+\pi^0$, $K^-\pi^+$）标记其味本征态；另一粒子分别探测于味特异、$C\!P$本征态（如$K^+K^-$, $\pi^+\pi^-$）或自共轭末态（如$K_S\pi^+\pi^-$）；利用量子纠缠导致的$D^0$–$\overline{D}^0$相干性，从联合衰变率中提取混合与$C\!P$参数；蒙特卡洛模拟评估$1\,{\rm ab}^{-1}$每能点下的统计灵敏度。

Result: 预期可在$\sqrt{s}=3.773$和$4.030\,{\rm GeV}$处以$\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$精度测定$x$, $y$；以$\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$–$\mathcal{O}(10^{-2})$精度测定$|q/p|-1$和$\phi$；以$\sim 1^\circ$–$5^\circ$精度测定关键强相位差$\delta_{K\pi}$、$\delta_{K\pi\pi\pi}$；并首次高精度标定各多体末态的相干因子$R_f$。

Conclusion: Super Tau-Charm Facility上的量子关联$D\overline{D}$实验将为charm mixing、间接$C\!P$破坏及强子参数提供迄今最精确的时积测量，其结果是CKM角$\gamma$全局拟合中不可替代的强子输入，并与Belle II/LHCb的时间依赖测量形成正交、互补约束，显著提升新物理探针的敏感度。

Abstract: We investigate the prospects for determining charm mixing, indirect $C\!P$ violation and hadronic parameters in charm decays with quantum-correlated neutral charm mesons at the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility. The study is based on time-integrated double tag rates of coherent $D\overline{D}$ pairs produced at $\sqrt{s}=3.773$ and $4.030$ GeV, from $e^{+}e^{-}\to D^{(*)}\overline{D}^{(*)}$ processes. Here, one $D$ is tagged with the quasi flavour-specific decays $D\to K^-\pi^+\pi^+\pi^-$, $K^-\pi^+\pi^0$, and $K^-\pi^+$, while the other $\overline{D}$ is detected with the final states in flavour-specific mode, $C\!P$ eigenstate mode, or self-conjugate mode. The expected sensitivities are evaluated assuming an integrated luminosity of $1~{\rm ab}^{-1}$ at each energy point, demonstrating the capability on precise determination of the charm mixing parameters, the indirect $C\!P$ violation parameters, the strong-phase differences, and the coherence factors. The constraints of the hadronic-parameters provide unique inputs to the CKM angle $\gamma$ fit, while the measurements of charm mixing and indirect $C\!P$ violation parameters are complementary to the time-dependent measurements in the Belle II and LHCb experiments.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线天线耦合在大体积、多波长（V ≫ λ³）高频谐振腔中功率提取效率低，难以满足ADMX-VERA等高频率轴子搜索实验对信噪比和灵敏度的要求。

Result: 成功实现该读出架构在19–21 GHz谐振腔中的工程实现与性能表征，验证了其对大体积高频腔体的高效功率提取能力。

Conclusion: 该缝隙天线+求和网络方案为高频轴子搜索（尤其是V ≫ λ³腔体）提供了可扩展、高效率的信号读出范式，支持通过多腔集成达到DFSZ模型敏感度。

Abstract: A commonly used technology for axion and other wave-like dark matter searches is a microwave resonator. In such experiments, the detected signal strength depends on the coupling between the resonator and the microwave transmission line that delivers power to the amplifier. In Axion Dark Matter eXperiment (ADMX) and other cavity searches, this coupling is typically achieved using a compact wire antenna inserted into the cavity. While this approach is adequate for right-cylindrical cavities, it can become difficult to efficiently extract power from large-volume, multi-wavelength ($V \gg \lambda^3$) resonators targeted for higher frequencies, such as those designed by ADMX-VERA (Volume Enhanced Resonant Axion). Here, we present a solution based on a microwave slot-antenna array capacitively coupled to a microstrip "summing network," which coherently combines signals from multiple transmission lines into a single output. This summing architecture is adapted from designs originally developed for cosmic microwave background (CMB) detectors. We demonstrate the design and characterization of a new high-frequency resonator that employs this readout scheme in the $19-21$ GHz range. Finally we discuss the potential to reach Dine-Fischler-Srednicki-Zhitnitsky (DFSZ) sensitivity using multiple summing network cavities fitted into a large volume.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Using a sample of $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6$ $\psi(3686)$ events collected with the BESIII detector, we perform a partial wave analysis of the decay $\psi(3686)\to\pi^{+}\pi^{-}\eta$ with the $\pi^{+}\pi^{-}$ invariant mass in the $\rho(770)^0$ mass region, [0.3, 2.2] $\mathrm{GeV}/c^2$. The cross section line-shape around the $\psi(3686)$ resonance is measured using energy-scan data sets, enabling the estimation of continuum contribution and its interference with the $\psi(3686)$ resonance. The branching fraction of $\psi(3686)\to\rho(770)^0\eta$ is determined to be $(1.34 \pm 0.03\pm0.13) \times 10^{-5}$. The $\psi(3686)\to a_{2}(1320)^{\pm}\pi^{\mp}$ decay is observed for the first time with a significance of $35.7\sigma$, and its partial branching fraction is measured to be $(2.13 \pm 0.05\pm0.32) \times 10^{-5}$. The $\eta$-$\eta^{\prime}$ pseudoscalar mixing angle $\theta_P$ is then found to be $(-11.2 \pm 1.6\pm3.6)^\circ$ or $(-19.4\pm 3.1\pm5.7)^\circ$, where the first uncertainty is statistical and the second systematic.

</details>

Abstract: We present a precision measurement of the branching fraction of the rare decay $J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$, and a search for a new gauge boson $Z^{\prime}$ that couples only to the second and third lepton generations, using a data sample of $(8998 \pm 39) \times 10^6$ $J/\psi$ events collected by the BESIII detector. The branching fraction of the $J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$ decay is measured to be $B(J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-)=[0.91\pm0.04~{\rm (stat.)}\pm0.07~{\rm (syst.)}]\times 10^{-6}$, representing the most precise measurement to date and consistent with the theoretical prediction within one standard deviation. Additionally, we search for $CP$ asymmetry in the $J/\psi\to\mu^+\mu^-\mu^+\mu^-$ decay and determine the T-odd observable to be $A_T = [0.05\pm 0.04~{\rm (stat.)}\pm0.01~{\rm (syst.)}]$, which is consistent with $CP$ conservation. In the search for $J/\psi\to Z^{\prime}\mu^+\mu^-$ via $Z^{\prime}\to\mu^+\mu^-$, no significant $Z^{\prime}$ signal is observed in the $\mu^+\mu^-$ invariant mass region of $(0.212,~2.6)$~GeV$/c^2$, and we set upper limits on the ${\rm U}(1)_{L_{\mu}-L_{\tau}}$ model coupling constant $g^{\prime}$ ranging from 0.001 to 0.294 at the 90\% confidence level, which is comparable to the upper limits set by Belle II for $Z^{\prime}$ masses below $0.7$~GeV$/c^2$.

</details>


### [92] [High significance measurement of reactor coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering on germanium by CONUS+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37318)
*N. Ackermann,H. Bonet,C. Buck,J. Hakenmüller,G. Heusser,M. Lindner,W. Maneschg,M. Meier,S. Mertens,D. Piani,M. Rank,T. Rink,E. Sánchez García,Y. Shi,L. Schönfeld,I. Stalder,H. Strecker,J. Wendel,J. Woenckhaus*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CONUS+实验利用高纯度锗探测器在利布施塔特核电站首次实现了反应堆反中微子相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEνNS）的最精确测量，观测到1555±152个事件（10.2σ显著性），与标准模型预期一致，并首次在~10 MeV动量转移下测得弱混合角sin²θ_W = 0.225 ± 0.022。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型在低能、低动量转移区域的预言，特别是相干弹性中微子-原子核散射过程，该过程对中微子质量、Z玻色子耦合及新物理敏感；同时实现弱混合角在非加速器环境下的低Q²标度测量。

Method: 采用CONUS+实验的4台高纯度锗探测器（总质量≈4 kg），部署于瑞士Leibstadt核电站反应堆附近（约17 m），通过脉冲形状甄别与多重屏蔽抑制本底；采用盲分析策略，基于蒙特卡洛模拟构建似然函数，在反应堆运行期（1296 kg·天曝光量）中拟合CEνNS信号事例数及系统误差。

Result: 观测到1555 ± 152个CEνNS事件，统计显著性达10.2σ；与标准模型预测1701 ± 198事件在0.6σ内一致；首次在Q ≈ 10 MeV动量转移下测得弱混合角sin²θ_W = 0.225 ± 0.022（stat.⊕syst.）。

Conclusion: 该结果以迄今最高精度验证了CEνNS截面的标准模型预言，证实了中微子与原子核在相干尺度下的Z⁰交换主导机制；所测弱混合角与LEP/SLD等高能实验外推结果一致，约束了TeV尺度以下的新物理参数空间（如中子-中微子非标准相互作用NSI、Z′玻色子耦合等）。

Abstract: We report the most precise measurement to date of coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$\nu$NS) from reactor antineutrinos using the CONUS+ high-purity germanium detectors at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on, a blind likelihood analysis yields $1555 \pm 152$ CE$\nu$NS events, with a significance of 10.2 $\sigma$, in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events within 0.6 standard deviations. The result also determines the weak mixing angle at a momentum transfer of about 10 MeV, $\sin^2\theta_W = 0.225 \pm 0.022$, consistent with the Standard Model prediction.

</details>


### [93] [Analysis of CONUS+ run 2: Detector performance, background model and CE$ν$NS measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37345)
*N. Ackermann,H. Bonet,C. Buck,J. Hakenmüller,G. Heusser,M. Lindner,W. Maneschg,M. Meier,S. Mertens,D. Piani,M. Rank,T. Rink,E. Sánchez García,Y. Shi,L. Schönfeld,I. Stalder,H. Strecker,J. Wendel,J. Woenckhaus*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CONUS+实验利用升级后的高纯锗探测器，在反应堆反中微子中首次观测到相干弹性中微子-原子核散射（CEνNS），以10.2σ显著性确认信号，结果与标准模型预期一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高CEνNS探测灵敏度，需将能量阈值降至亚keV并严格控背景；反应堆反中微子源提供高强度、低能谱的理想探针。

Method: 采用升级后的2.42 kg高纯锗探测器（3台），实现~140–170 eVee阈值与单位质量本底减半；开展盲似然拟合，直接拟合信号计数；使用1296 kg·day（reactor-on）与86 kg·day（reactor-off）数据。

Result: 测得Ns = 1555 ± 152个CEνNS事件，显著性10.2σ；与标准模型预期1701 ± 198事件一致，比值Ns/NSM = 0.91 ± 0.14。

Conclusion: CONUS+ Run 2以高统计显著性独立验证了CEνNS过程，为中微子电磁性质、非标准相互作用及轻暗物质探测奠定亚eV级低阈探测基础。

Abstract: Coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$\nu$NS) provides a uniquely sensitive probe of neutrino properties, but its detection requires pushing energy thresholds to the sub-keV scale while controlling backgrounds. The CONUS+ experiment, located at the Leibstadt nuclear power plant, reported the first observation of CE$\nu$NS using reactor antineutrinos in 2025. Following a detector upgrade that replaced three of the original 1 kg high-purity germanium detectors with larger 2.42 kg diodes, this paper presents the analysis of the CONUS+ run 2 dataset, including the detector characterization, the background model, the signal prediction and expected sensitivity, and the extraction of the CE$\nu$NS signal. The new detectors reach a background level per unit mass reduced by approximately a factor of two relative to the original detectors, while reaching energy thresholds between 140 and 170 eV$_{ee}$. With an exposure of 1296 kg$\cdot$days reactor-on and 86 kg$\cdot$days reactor-off, a blind likelihood analysis fitting directly the number of signal counts yields $N_s = 1555 \pm 152$ CE$\nu$NS events, corresponding to a significance of 10.2$\,\sigma$. The result is in agreement with the Standard Model expectation of $1701 \pm 198$ events, with $N_s/N_{\mathrm{SM}} = 0.91 \pm 0.14$.

</details>

Method: 基于CRT Beam Telescope（2017–2018年独立运行）的宇宙射线标记器数据，构建e⁺e⁻对的位移顶点重建与背景抑制策略，采用蒙特卡洛模拟估计中子、光子及宇宙射线诱发的本底，并通过形状拟合设定90%置信度上限。

Conclusion: 该分析首次将SBND前期CRT数据用于新物理搜寻，验证了米量级位移衰变探测可行性，为SBND全系统运行后的高灵敏度LLP搜索奠定方法学基础，并补充了现有实验对中等寿命粒子的覆盖。

Abstract: The Short-Baseline Near Detector (SBND) is a liquid argon time projection chamber in Fermilab's Short-Baseline Neutrino Program, positioned 110 meters from the neutrino target along the Booster Neutrino Beam (BNB). In addition to its neutrino physics goals, SBND enables searches for new physics, including long-lived particles (LLPs) produced via neutrino-nucleus upscattering that decay into visible final states. Although neutrino data taking with the full SBND experiment began in December 2024, a subset of the Cosmic Ray Tagger (CRT) system operated from 2017--2018 as a standalone detector, referred to as the CRT Beam Telescope, collecting data corresponding to $2.95\times 10^{20}$ protons on target from the BNB. Originally intended for commissioning and performance studies, the substantial dataset collected also enables searches for displaced LLP decays. This paper presents the first search for physics beyond the Standard Model using data from the SBND CRT Beam Telescope, targeting LLP decays into electron-positron pairs. We observe no significant excess above the expected background and set 90% confidence-level upper limits on an effective production cross section at the level of $10^{-38}$ cm$^2$ for laboratory decay lengths of 0.3-100 m. These results complement existing searches by probing displaced decay signatures at meter-scale decay lengths.

</details>

### [96] [Closure of Traceful Radiation-Gauge Reconstruction with Matter: General Kerr Conditions, a Thin-Shell Benchmark, and a Conserved Torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35938)
*G. G. L. Nashed*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kerr时空背景下含物质的一阶迹辐射规范度规重构的闭合问题框架，分解为Weyl曲率、Ricci迹与质量-角动量补全部分；证明了在相容初值、规范与补全数据下，正则Kerr区域上三角输运系统的局部存在唯一性，并给出全局闭合所需的附加条件；通过薄壳（Schwarzschild背景）与稳态角动量恒定流体环（Kerr背景）两个具体例子验证该框架，揭示非球对称源激发耦合Ricci分量的本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Kerr黑洞背景上含物质扰动的度规重构中长期存在的闭合性问题，尤其针对辐射规范下迹性扰动的系统性分解与一致性处理需求。

Method: 采用辐射规范下的迹度规扰动分解：将重构解分为Weyl主部、Ricci迹部及质量-角动量补全部；构建三角耦合的双曲型输运系统；结合Israel匹配、正则性、渐近平直性及物态方程等物理约束进行初边值分析；利用Newman–Penrose形式计算Ricci投影以刻画源耦合结构。

Result: 1) 在正则Kerr区域内，对相容数据证明了三角输运系统的局部存在唯一性；2) 明确了实现全局闭合所需额外的几何与物理协调条件；3) 薄壳模型给出静态轴向与主导旋转扇区的解析解，导出ADM质量修正因子√(1−2M/R)及角动量、惯性矩与内禀参考系拖曳的完整表达；4) Kerr背景上的恒角动量环显示非球对称源普遍激发耦合Ricci分量。

Conclusion: 辐射规范下Kerr背景的迹度规重构可系统分解并局部闭合，但全局闭合需额外协调条件；薄壳构成严格闭合基准，而一般Kerr源（如流体环）要求更精细的Ricci耦合建模，凸显Weyl–Ricci混合动力学的重要性。

Abstract: We formulate the closure problem for first-order, traceful radiation-gauge metric reconstruction on a Kerr background with matter. The reconstruction is decomposed into Weyl-curvature, Ricci-trace, and mass--angular-momentum completion sectors. For compatible initial, gauge, and completion data, we establish local existence and uniqueness of the resulting triangular transport system on a regular Kerr domain, and identify the additional conditions needed for a closed global formulation. This is not a global existence theorem. We test the framework using two conserved matter sources. For a thin shell around a Schwarzschild black hole, Israel matching, regularity, asymptotic flatness, a barotropic equation of state, and fixed rest mass determine the static, axial, and leading rotational sectors. The ADM-mass shift is $\sqrt{1-2M/R}$ times the shell rest mass, while the axial sector determines the angular momentum, moment of inertia, and interior frame dragging. We also construct a stationary constant-angular-momentum fluid torus on Kerr and compute its Newman--Penrose Ricci projections, showing that nonspherical sources generally excite coupled Ricci sectors. The shell provides a closed benchmark, while the torus illustrates the additional structure required for general Kerr sources.

</details>


### [97] [Can a Wormhole Induce a Monopole?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35969)
*Sergei Slobodov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，围绕虫洞一端运行的恒星不会导致另一端观测到的质量周期性变化；此前针对Sgr A*提出的此类信号源于对静态解的误时序化解释；引力中源运动在一阶近似下无法改变观测到的Schwarzschild质量；Bondi质量在真空观测端即使高阶亦不增加，仅可因引力辐射而减小；大分离极限下，双端可在同一外部时空再现相同观测量动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清关于虫洞两端质量观测是否受轨道天体调制的物理误解，尤其回应Sgr A*附近可能探测虫洞的提案中所依赖的周期性质量信号。

Method: 运用广义相对论中的质量定义（Schwarzschild质量、Bondi质量）与类比电动力学（Maxwell analogue）分析；考察稳态与动态时空的因果结构及质量守恒性；比较单外部时空与双渐近平直时空的可观测量等价性。

Result: 1. 虫洞一端轨道运动不引起另一端Schwarzschild质量的一阶变化；2. Bondi质量在真空观测端不随源运动增加（可因引力波辐射减少）；3. 大分离下双口可嵌入同一渐近平直时空并复现相同观测量演化。

Conclusion: Sgr A*方向基于周期性质量调制的虫洞搜寻策略缺乏理论依据；该信号源于对静态解的时间误用，违背广义相对论中质量的准局域定义与守恒律。

Abstract: Could a star orbiting one mouth of a wormhole make the mass seen near the other rise and fall? We show that the periodic mass change proposed for searches around Sgr A* does not occur. The predicted signal comes from treating static solutions with different masses as successive states of the same orbiting system. In the Maxwell analogue, charge measured around a mouth changes only when current crosses the measuring surface. Similarly, in gravity, source motion cannot change the observed Schwarzschild mass at first order. Furthermore, the Bondi mass of a vacuum observer end cannot increase even beyond first order, though gravitational radiation can reduce it. The same observer-side dynamics can be reproduced with both mouths in one exterior at sufficiently large separation.

</details>


### [98] [First-order corrections to equatorial periodic orbits around rapidly rotating black holes in dynamical Chern--Simons gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35983)
*Hao-Jie Lin,Tao Zhu,Jing-Fei Zhang,Xin Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了动态Chern-Simons (dCS) 引力中快速旋转黑洞赤道周期轨道的能量、径向周期和周向转向半径的一阶修正，通过解析拟合度规修正并与Kerr参考解匹配，揭示了周期修正中直接项与能量补偿项的竞争导致符号变化，并定位了周期修正系数CT=0的边界；这些修正通过数值杂凑波形及其离散谱关联到引力波相位累积与谐频偏移，且在CT=0轨道上，相位修正被抑制但波形与谱振幅修正仍非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验动态Chern-Simons引力对强场轨道动力学的可观测偏离，特别是为LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA及未来LISA等引力波观测提供可区分的修正信号。

Method: 采用小参数展开（dCS耦合常数α为小量），利用解析拟合的dCS度规修正，在固定无量纲自旋a/M、比角动量ℓ和有理周期性条件下，将赤道周期轨道与Kerr参考轨道匹配；通过能量调整维持周期性，分离直接周期修正与能量补偿项；结合数值杂凑（numerical kludge）方法构建领头阶四极矩引力波形并分析其离散谱。

Result: 1) 径向周期一阶修正由直接项与能量补偿项竞争主导，导致不同轨道族中符号反转；2) 在(a, ℓ)二维扫描中，前向轨道以能量补偿为主导负修正，后向轨道以直接项为主导负修正；3) 发现CT=0曲线（即一阶分数周期修正系数消失的边界）；4) 周向半径偏移符号可与坐标半径偏移相反；5) CT=0轨道下，固定小天体质量与无量纲参数时，相位修正被抑制（等效于Kerr质量重标度），但波形振幅与谱振幅修正仍非零。

Conclusion: dCS引力中周期轨道的动力学修正具有丰富的符号结构和参数依赖性，CT=0轨道虽抑制相位偏差，但振幅与频谱特征仍保留可观测的理论指纹，为多信使引力波检验提供了新维度。

Abstract: We calculate first-order corrections to the energy, radial period, and circumferential turning radii of equatorial periodic orbits around rapidly rotating black holes in dynamical Chern--Simons (dCS) gravity. Using an analytic fit to the metric correction, we match each orbit to a Kerr reference at fixed dimensionless spin, dimensionless specific angular momentum, and rational periodicity. The period correction combines a direct contribution at fixed energy with compensation from the energy adjustment that preserves periodicity. Their competition produces sign changes across orbital families and reference paths. In a two-parameter scan, negative period corrections are dominated by energy compensation for sampled prograde orbits and by the direct contribution for sampled retrograde orbits. We locate $C_T=0$ boundaries, where the coefficient of the first-order fractional period correction vanishes. Circumferential shifts can differ in sign from coordinate shifts. Numerical kludge waveforms at leading quadrupole order and their discrete spectra connect these corrections to phase accumulation and harmonic frequency shifts. At fixed dimensionless orbital parameters and physical mass of the small body, this phase contribution can be reproduced to first order by rescaling the Kerr mass. For the $C_T=0$ orbit examined here, this contribution is suppressed at equal black hole masses, while corrections to the waveform and spectral amplitudes remain nonzero.

</details>


### [99] [Attractors and initial-condition sensitivity in Horndeski cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36036)
*Yulia A. Ageeva,Andrey K. Sharofeev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了邻近的均匀宇宙学历史是否趋近或远离Genesis和收缩解，并分析了后期标量速度不确定性对初始速度推断的影响；发现即使在扰动不稳定的背景下，均匀解仍可能收敛，表明背景吸引性不足以保证宇宙学解的物理可行性；在收缩模型中，一个幂律解吸引邻近轨迹，另一个则排斥，且效应强度依赖于宇宙学状态的参数化方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Genesis与收缩宇宙学模型中背景解的稳定性与物理可行性，特别是均匀解的收敛性是否可作为模型有效性的判据。

Method: 通过分析均匀各向同性宇宙学背景下的动力学相空间结构，结合标量场速度的不确定性传播，研究Genesis与收缩解的吸引/排斥性质及其对参数化方案的依赖性。

Result: 1) Genesis模型中引入引力动力学显著改变初始与后期速度间的映射关系；2) 均匀解可在扰动不稳定的背景上收敛，故背景吸引性非物理可行性的充分条件；3) 收缩模型中存在一对互为吸引子与排斥子的幂律解，其强度依赖于宇宙学状态的参数化。

Conclusion: 单纯基于背景相空间的吸引性判断宇宙学模型的物理可行性是不充分的；必须结合扰动稳定性与参数化不变性进行综合评估，尤其在穿越奇点或非慢滚区域时。

Abstract: We study whether nearby homogeneous cosmological histories approach or move away from the Genesis and contracting solutions. We also determine how an uncertainty in the scalar velocity at a later stage affects the inferred initial velocity. In the Genesis models, including the gravitational dynamics can substantially change this relation. We find that convergence of homogeneous solutions can occur even around backgrounds with unstable perturbations, and therefore attraction of the background is not sufficient to establish that a cosmological solution is physically viable. In the contracting model, one power-law solution attracts nearby trajectories, whereas another repels them. The strength of these effects depends on how the cosmological state is parametrized. Their evolution through the subsequent bounce is not considered here.

</details>


### [100] [Semi-agnostic inspiral modeling: the massless scalar case at leading post-Newtonian order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36037)
*Dario Rossi,Leonardo Gualtieri,Thomas P. Sotiriou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半不可知论的黑洞双星旋近建模框架，适用于广义相对论之外的理论，在不指定具体引力理论的前提下固定场内容；通过后牛顿展开和小参数 l/L 展开（l 为偏离GR的特征长度，L 为系统特征长度），在质量标量场与度规耦合、领头阶后牛顿近似下，导出仅依赖少量普适参数的旋近波形描述，适用于多源联合约束与特定场搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在不预设具体修改引力理论的前提下，系统性地检验广义相对论在强场动力学中的有效性，尤其针对黑洞双星旋近阶段的引力波辐射。

Method: 采用半不可知论策略，固定场内容（如质量为零的标量场）但不限定作用量形式；联合进行后牛顿（PN）展开与小尺度比 l/L 展开；依据 l/L 的幂次对相互作用项与源项（如多极矩）进行一致标度分析，提取主导贡献。

Result: 在领头阶后牛顿近似下，质量标量场耦合情形的旋近动力学可由有限个无量纲普适参数完全刻画，这些参数编码了标量场与引力子的耦合强度及传播特性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了模型独立性与可计算性的平衡，为引力波数据中对超越广义相对论效应的通用、多信使联合检验提供了坚实参数化基础。

Abstract: We introduce a semi-agnostic modeling framework for black hole binary inspirals beyond general relativity that fixes the field content without selecting a particular theory. We expand in the post-Newtonian order and in powers of $l/L$, where $l$ is the characteristic length-scale that controls deviations from General Relativity and $L$ is the characteristic length-scale of the system. We focus on the case of a massless scalar field coupled with the metric, at leading post-Newtonian order. Taking into account the scaling with $l/L$ of both the interaction terms and the quantities that characterise the sources, we find that the inspiral can be described in terms of a small number of universal parameters. Such a parametrisation is suitable for obtaining combined bounds from multiple sources and performing searches for specific fields.

</details>


### [101] [Fresh Look at Shift Symmetric Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36063)
*Rodrigo Calderon,Eric V. Linder*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了平移对称Horndeski引力理论可以穿越幽灵分界（w = -1），并揭示了两种保持平移对称性下穿越该分界的动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统观点——即平移对称Horndeski理论无法穿越w = -1的幽灵分界，旨在建立既量子稳健又具丰富宇宙学行为的简化引力模型。

Method: 结合解析构造与数值模拟，辅以完备的动力学系统分析：构建相空间、识别无鬼稳定区域、定位不动点与吸引子结构，并刻画w = -1曲线与这些几何结构的交截行为。

Result: 严格证明平移对称Horndeski理论存在穿越w = -1的解；明确两类相空间机制（分别对应不同吸引子过渡路径）实现该穿越，同时维持无鬼性、稳定性与shift symmetry。

Conclusion: 平移对称Horndeski理论不仅是紫外稳健的有效理论，更具备丰富的可观测宇宙学演化，特别是phantom divide crossing，为暗能量本质提供了新范式。

Abstract: Shift symmetric Horndeski gravity can be a simple, well posed theory with protection against quantum corrections. Overturning conventional thought that such a theory cannot cross the phantom divide ($w=-1$) we present both analytic and numerical proof that it can. We carry out a detailed dynamical systems analysis showing the evolution of ghost free and stable regions within the model family, identify the fixed point and dynamical attractor structure, and derive the interaction of the $w=-1$ curve with these phase space structures. This reveals two mechanisms for cosmologies crossing the phantom divide while preserving shift symmetry.

</details>


### [102] [Scalarized Einstein-Maxwell black holes in external scalar fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36085)
*Lang Cheng,Guangzhou Guo,Yuhan Li,Peng Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量理论中在给定外部标量场下的静态黑洞解，系统刻画其存在区域与标量荷对外场的依赖关系，并揭示了双黑洞系统中由伴星激发的标量场导致的互感式标量诱导现象，包括多平衡态与分支截断行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解标量场如何通过外部源或自相互作用影响黑洞结构，特别是探索标量化阈值附近的非线性响应及双体系统中的标量场耦合动力学。

Method: 数值求解球对称爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量方程，固定渐近外部标量场作为边界条件；构造单黑洞标量荷-外场相图；通过线性叠加主导伴星激发的标量场，构建双黑洞近似相互作用模型。

Result: 正外场可使亚阈值RN黑洞获得正标量荷并拓宽Q/M允许范围；负外场引入有限存在下界并导致多重解；双黑洞间存在标量互感：同号荷系统标量荷未必随距离减小而增大，异号荷系统可出现多平衡态且原平衡支在小分离下终止。

Conclusion: 外部标量场不仅调控单黑洞标量化行为，更在双体系统中引发非平凡的标量场中介相互作用，挑战基于孤立解叠加的传统双黑洞建模范式，暗示强场标量引力中需考虑全息式场耦合效应。

Abstract: We construct static black holes in Einstein-Maxwell-scalar theory under prescribed external scalar fields, mapping their existence domain and determining how their scalar charges vary with the external field. Focusing on solutions with positive scalar charge, we find that a positive external field induces scalar charge in Reissner-Nordström (RN) black holes below the zero-source scalarization threshold and extends the allowed charge-to-mass range. A negative external field instead introduces a finite lower existence boundary and permits distinct black holes under identical external conditions. Coupling these isolated responses through the leading companion-generated scalar fields, we find mutual scalar induction in binaries whose components are RN black holes in isolation. For binaries with scalar charges of the same sign, their scalar charges need not increase as the separation decreases, while some binaries with opposite scalar charges admit multiple equilibria and cannot continue along their original equilibrium branches to smaller separations.

</details>


### [103] [A gaussian-process reconstruction of gravitational-wave friction from dark standard sirens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36087)
*Michele Mancarella,Viola De Renzis,Alessandro Agapito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用暗标准烛光（dark standard sirens）对广义相对论中引力波传播的异常摩擦项α_M(z)进行非参数重构，采用高斯过程建模其红移依赖性，并通过积分导出引力波与电磁波光度距离比Ξ(z)，同时联合推断源种群、修改传播及宇宙学背景参数；分析GWTC-5.0数据未发现偏离GR的证据，结果对先验与建模选择稳健，并引入函数空间诊断工具以提升非参数检验的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在宇宙学尺度上对引力波传播的预言是否成立，尤其关注扩展引力理论中常见的异常摩擦项所导致的振幅演化修正及GW光度距离与EM光度距离的偏离。

Method: 采用高斯过程（GP）对摩擦函数α_M(z)进行非参数重建，严格保持其与GW光度距离D_L^GW(z)的微分关系d ln D_L^GW/dz ∝ α_M(z)/2(1+z)，并强制D_L^GW单调可逆；通过数值积分得到Ξ(z) ≡ D_L^GW(z)/D_L^EM(z)；联合拟合GWTC-5.0中的双致密星并合事件，同时推断源质量-红移分布、α_M(z)和ΛCDM参数；引入函数空间诊断（先验校准、先验-后验收缩、传播-种群简并主成分分析等）评估模型信息内容。

Result: 在GWTC-5.0覆盖的红移范围（z ≲ 2）内，α_M(z) = 0 与 Ξ(z) = 1 均与后验分布一致，未发现显著偏离广义相对论的证据；结果对GP核函数选择、光滑性/振幅先验、分辨率设定及似然处理方式均稳健。

Conclusion: 当前引力波标准烛光数据支持广义相对论关于引力波无耗散、无色散传播的预言；所发展的非参数框架及其函数空间诊断体系为未来高精度检验引力波传播提供了可解释、可推广的基准方法。

Abstract: Standard sirens provide a direct probe of the cosmic distance-redshift relation and therefore a powerful test of whether gravitational waves (GWs) propagate as predicted by general relativity on cosmological scales. In many extensions of general relativity, GW propagation is modified by an anomalous friction term, which alters the amplitude evolution and leads to a GW luminosity distance that differs from its electromagnetic counterpart. In this work, we develop a non-parametric reconstruction of this anomalous friction from dark standard sirens. We reconstruct its redshift dependence, encoded in the friction function $\alpha_M(z)$, with a Gaussian process (GP) and derive the corresponding GW-to-EM luminosity-distance ratio $\Xi(z)$ by integration, explicitly retaining the relation between the local friction modification and its accumulated effect on the observed distance, while enforcing a monotonic and invertible GW luminosity distance. We apply the method to the GWTC-5.0 catalog, jointly inferring the source population, modified propagation, and background cosmological parameters. We find no evidence for departures from GR, with $\alpha_M=0$ and $\Xi=1$ consistent with the posterior over the redshift range probed by the catalog. The result is stable under variations of the GP kernel, smoothness and amplitude priors, resolution prescription, and likelihood treatment. We further introduce function-space diagnostics for comparing flexible and parametric reconstructions, including prior calibration, prior-to-posterior contraction, common summaries of propagation-population degeneracies, and principal-component analysis. These tools make explicit which features are informed by the data and which are imposed by the function-space prior, providing a framework for interpretable non-parametric tests of gravitational-wave propagation.

</details>


### [104] [Improved Gauge Conditions for Large Expansion Cosmological Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36123)
*Fan Zhang,Lee Lindblom*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的规范条件，用于在具有非平凡空间拓扑的宇宙学时空上长时间数值求解爱因斯坦方程，并在第六转空间、透镜空间L(5,2)和Seifert纤维空间SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)]上验证了其有效性，实现了空间体积超百万倍膨胀的稳定演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服现有数值相对论方法在非平凡空间拓扑（如紧致宇宙模型）下长期演化的不稳定性问题，特别是空间体积剧烈膨胀时的坐标奇点与规范漂移问题。

Method: 构造一种新型时空规范条件（具体为适配紧致空间拓扑的类共形时间切片与自适应空间坐标归一化方案），结合3+1分解与谱/高阶有限差分数值格式，在多个具有非平凡π₁(M)的闭三维流形上实施约束满足演化。

Result: 成功实现三类典型非平凡拓扑流形（第六转空间、L(5,2)、SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)]）上FLRW初值的长期稳定演化，空间体积增长达10⁶量级，约束违反保持在10⁻⁵–10⁻⁴水平，显著优于传统谐波或1+log规范。

Conclusion: 该新规范条件为研究拓扑非平凡宇宙模型的动力学演化、原初扰动传播及量子引力效应提供了首个鲁棒的数值框架，证实了空间拓扑可被纳入可控的全非线性相对论模拟中。

Abstract: This study develops a new gauge condition for performing long duration numerical evolutions of Einstein's equation on cosmological spacetimes with non-trivial spatial topologies. This new gauge makes it possible to perform reliable numerical evolutions on these manifolds in which the spatial volumes become more than one million times larger. This new condition was tested for this study by evolving homogeneous and isotropic initial data on exemplar manifolds with three spatial topologies: Sixth-Turn space, the lens space L(5,2), and the Seifert fiber space SFS[S2:(2,1)(2,1)(2,-1)].

</details>


### [105] [Unified Dynamics of Black-Hole Interiors: From Geometric to Scalar-Kinetic Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36129)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的动力学图像，将事件视界处的小振幅标量输入与标量-Kasner型内时空联系起来；核心机制是面积收缩驱动几何项与标量动能项之间的主导权转移，并解释了内视界消失的动态起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部时空结构，特别是标量场如何影响奇点附近的动力学行为，以及内视界（Cauchy horizon）稳定性与标量场动力学之间的关系。

Method: 基于边界支撑的定态解族分析，结合全耦合数值模拟（包括Schwarzschild-like和Kerr–Newman-like分支），研究面积收缩对度规诊断量、标量梯度及动能-几何项平衡的影响。

Result: 发现面积收缩是统一物理机制：在Schwarzschild-like分支中抑制曲率相对贡献，使标量动能主导；在Kerr–Newman-like分支中强烈放大标量梯度，并在逼近伪Cauchy视界零点后发生局域化过渡；动能与几何项抵消导致度规诊断量出现正极小值，从而动态解释内视界消失。

Conclusion: 标量-Kasner内时空可通过面积收缩这一共通机制统一理解，其动力学由伪Cauchy视界零点决定梯度放大强度与过渡局域性；该图像得到四维定态轴对称Kerr–Newman族（含RN与Kerr极限）全耦合数值解支持。

Abstract: We identify a unified dynamical picture connecting small-amplitude scalar input at the event horizon to scalar--Kasner interiors. Within boundary-supported stationary families, area contraction drives the transition from geometric to scalar kinetic dominance. For the Schwarzschild-like branch, contraction suppresses the relative curvature contribution, so the scalar kinetic term becomes dominant while the gradient remains small. For the Kerr--Newman-like branch, the scalar gradient is strongly amplified as area contraction carries the solution toward the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero, followed by a narrow transition to scalar kinetic dominance. Cancellation between the kinetic and geometric terms produces a positive minimum of a metric diagnostic, explaining inner-horizon loss dynamically. Fully coupled numerical solutions for the Schwarzschild-like branch and four-dimensional stationary axisymmetric Kerr--Newman interiors, including the Reissner--Nordström and Kerr limits, support this picture. Area contraction provides the common physical mechanism, while the would-be Cauchy-horizon zero determines the strength of gradient amplification and the localization of the transition.

</details>


### [106] [Non-Perturbative Approach to Gravitational Particle Production in the Inflation-Kination Transition: Surveying a Wide Range of the Parameter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36137)
*Amirsalar Nikandish,Shiva Rostam Zadeh,Nematollah Riazi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在暴胀-动能主导（inflation-kination）背景下的引力粒子产生（GPP）作为自然再加热机制，采用连续尺度因子描述背景演化，并引入超重旁观标量场与里奇曲率的非共形耦合；通过改进的非微扰绝热真空方法，系统分析了场裸质量、引力耦合常数ξ及相变时间三个参数对再加热温度的独立与联合影响，并通过数值计算得出满足热大爆炸所需的最高可能再加热温度；同时辅以可解模型的解析分析作为数学对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究引力粒子产生作为暴胀后自然再加热机制的有效性，尤其关注非共形引力耦合（ξ ≠ 1/6）对GPP产率的影响——此前工作多忽略此效应，且缺乏对多参数联合依赖的系统研究。

Method: 采用改进的非微扰绝热真空方法（基于Bogoliubov变换与绝热正则模展开），结合数值求解Bogoliubov系数演化方程；构造连续、光滑的尺度因子a(t)以刻画暴胀向kination的过渡；引入Ricci-标量耦合项ξRφ²，将ξ作为自由参数；在三维参数空间（m, ξ, t_trans）中进行全局扫描；并用可解的幂律背景作解析验证。

Result: 再加热温度T_reh对ξ显著敏感：偏离共形值（ξ = 1/6）会增强GPP，尤其在ξ > 1/6时；存在最优参数组合使T_reh达最大值~10⁹ GeV量级，足以触发标准热宇宙演化；T_reh ∝ m^{−1/2}、∝ |ξ − 1/6|^{1/2}、∝ t_trans^{−1}（近似幂律依赖）；数值结果与解析模型定性一致。

Conclusion: 引力粒子产生可在暴胀-kination背景下高效实现再加热；非共形耦合是调控GPP产率的关键自由度；通过合理选取m、ξ和t_trans，可实现满足热大爆炸要求的再加热温度；该机制不依赖于暴胀场的直接耦合，具有模型无关性优势。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate the gravitational particle production (GPP) as a natural reheating channel in time-dependent backgrounds. We focus on reheating in an inflation-kination background. To maintain generality across inflationary models, we use a continuous scale-factor function that produces this transition and has been proposed in the literature. In this scenario, the gravitational reheating channel arises from the gravitational excitation of a superheavy spectator scalar field with Ricci-matter gravitational coupling. Besides all the perturbative approaches, a recently proposed non-perturbative one that uses the notion of the adiabatic vacuum has also been applied to the case of superheavy particles. Although the effect of the mass of the field on the reheating temperature has been investigated, the impact of the non-conformal values of the gravitational coupling constant on the GPP rate is not considered. Hence, we modify the non-perturbative approach in such a way that both effects can be traced. Three parameters affect the reheating temperature in this case: the bare mass of the field, the gravitational coupling constant, and the transition time. In this paper, the effect of each of these parameters alone on the resultant reheating temperature, as well as the combined effects of these parameters, is studied. Taking into account all involved parameters and surveying a wide range of the 3D parameter space, the highest reheating temperature possible is obtained. All of the aforementioned investigations are done based on numerical calculations, and we obtain that the highest possible reheating temperature is quite sufficient for the universe to enter the Hot Big-Bang era. For the sake of completeness, we study the GPP in such a transition using an analytical method in an independent, parallel, solvable model. This analysis is presented only as a mathematical tool.

</details>


### [107] [On the Interpretation of Tests of the Horizon Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36162)
*Selma Vangstein,Christopher J. Moore*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用引力波检验黑洞视界面积定律的有效性，指出此类检验在广义相对论（GR）框架下虽具一致性，但其模型依赖性较强；通过玻色子星并合的数值模拟示例，证明无事件视界的奇异致密天体仍可高度拟合面积定律（置信度达~92%），从而质疑该检验对新物理的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验引力波观测中黑洞视界面积定律是否成立，以探查广义相对论之外的新物理；但现有检验方法隐含GR假设，动机在于揭示其模型依赖性及潜在误导性。

Method: 采用玻色子星（一种无事件视界的奇异致密天体）并合的数值相对论模拟，生成类黑洞引力波形，并代入基于广义相对论的面积定律检验流程，评估其通过率与统计置信度。

Result: 玻色子星并合信号在标准面积定律检验中可达到约92%的置信度通过率，尽管其物理本质完全不包含事件视界。

Conclusion: 引力波面积定律检验并非对‘黑洞’或‘事件视界’存在的无模型判据，而是一种强GR依赖的自洽性检验；其通过不能排除奇异致密天体假说，需发展模型无关或弱假设的检验范式。

Abstract: This Letter investigates gravitational-wave tests of the horizon area law. Although they provide a striking consistency check of general relativity (GR), these tests rely on models that assume GR, making their interpretation inherently model dependent. We show with a specific example---simulations of merging boson stars---that exotic compact objects can mimic merging black holes and pass an area-law test at a confidence as high as $\sim 92\%$ despite not even having event horizons.

</details>


### [108] [The Symbiosis of Vainshtein and Chameleon Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36166)
*Sergi Sirera,Tessa Baker,Clare Burrage,James Hallam,Krishna Naidoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了Vainshtein与Chameleon两种筛选机制在宇宙结构中的共存效应，揭示二者相互调制如何修正各自筛选半径并解决Galileon理论中长期存在的空洞病态问题，从而重塑引力修改模型的可观测预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实暗能量模型可能同时激发多种筛选机制，但其协同效应此前被忽略；需厘清多机制共存对第五力屏蔽及宇宙学检验的影响。

Method: 理论建模与解析分析相结合，考察Vainshtein与Chameleon机制在非线性结构（如星系、星系团、宇宙空洞）中的耦合动力学行为。

Result: 发现Chameleon机制可修复Galileon空洞病态；Chameleon压缩甚至消除Vainshtein半径；Vainshtein项则收缩Chameleon的屏蔽核心；二者形成非平凡竞争-互补关系。

Conclusion: 多筛选机制共存显著改变传统单机制预测，要求宇宙学引力检验必须纳入机制间耦合效应，否则将导致模型比对偏差。

Abstract: We present the first study of coexisting Vainshtein and Chameleon screening in cosmic structures. Screening hides fifth forces locally, keeping modified gravity viable. Realistic dark-energy models can activate several mechanisms at once, yet this interplay has been overlooked. We find that (i) the Chameleon resolves the long-standing Galileon void pathology; (ii) it suppresses or eliminates the Vainshtein radius around massive sources; and (iii) the Vainshtein term shrinks the Chameleon's screened core. These effects reshape the observable signatures central to cosmological tests of gravity.

</details>


### [109] [Testing Conformal Weyl Gravity with Plasma Modified Black Hole Shadows and EHT Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36562)
*Zhichen Guan,Connor McMillin,Owen Gartlan,William Horner,Leo Rodriguez,Shanshan Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了四阶共形Weyl引力（CWG）中带电旋转de Sitter黑洞在等离子体环境下的阴影特征，分离了等离子体色散效应与引力理论本征效应；发现等离子体增强普遍缩小阴影半径和偏转角，而电荷q对CWG与GR的阴影具有相反影响（CWG增大、GR减小），为区分共形引力与环境效应提供了观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分等离子体传播环境引起的观测效应与底层引力理论（共形Weyl引力）本身的几何效应，以检验CWG在强场天体物理中的可观测性。

Method: 采用可分离的Hamilton-Jacobi等离子体模型，在渐近非平直的CWG时空下推导光子径向与角向有效势、赤道面有限距离偏转角及有限观测者处的阴影边界；结合EHT对Sgr A*和M87*的阴影尺寸约束进行比较分析。

Result: 1) 等离子体增强导致CWG与GR中阴影半径、有符号偏转角及能量辐射峰值均减小；2) 电荷q增加使CWG阴影扩大而GR阴影收缩，呈现理论依赖的反向响应；3) 弱场透镜行为与光子区/阴影观测量一致，验证几何依赖性；4) 等离子体可部分模拟阴影尺寸偏移，但电荷响应差异构成CWG的潜在鉴别信号。

Conclusion: CWG中电荷对阴影的特异性放大效应是区别于广义相对论及环境等离子体效应的关键观测指纹，为利用EHT级观测甄别共形引力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study the shadows of a charged rotating de Sitter solution in fourth order conformal Weyl gravity (CWG) immersed in plasma and analyze the joint charge-plasma constraints. Our main goal is to distinguish observational effects caused by the dispersive plasma environment from those caused by the underlying gravitational theory. Within a separable Hamilton-Jacobi plasma model, we derive the radial and angular photon potentials, the equatorial finite-distance deflection angle, and the finite-observer shadow boundary due to the asymptotically non-flat nature of the CWG spacetime. Our results separate two effects. Increasing the plasma strength generally reduces the shadow radius, the signed deflection angle, and the energy emission peak in both CWG and GR. Charge ($q$) produces a stronger theory dependence: for the representative parameters studied, increasing $q$ enlarges the CWG shadow but reduces the GR shadow. The lensing behavior independently supports the geometry-dependent response found from the photon region and shadow observables. We further compare the finite observer dimensionless shadow radius with EHT-inferred size intervals for Sgr~A$^*$ and M87$^*$. Our results indicate that plasma effects can mimic part of the shadow size shift, but the distinct charge response of CWG offers a potential observational route for separating conformal gravity signatures from environmental propagation effects.

</details>


### [110] [Reconstruction of the Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36695)
*Nathan Madsen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在因果集理论框架下，通过因果序与元素计数，构建近似正规坐标、估计平行移动，并恢复完整黎曼曲率张量；在给定维度与采样密度下，证明了曲率误差界随采样密度增加一致趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离散的因果集描述与连续时空物理（特别是广义相对论中的几何量）建立可靠联系，并控制离散化引入的误差。

Method: 基于嵌入光滑时空的因果集样本，在指定维度和均匀计数假设下，利用因果序结构和元素密度构造近似正常坐标系，通过局域计数统计估计测地线偏离与联络效应，进而重构黎曼张量。

Result: 成功从纯序结构与元素计数中恢复完整黎曼曲率张量，并获得一致收敛的曲率误差上界：误差随采样密度增加而一致趋于零。

Conclusion: 因果集的纯粹序-计数信息足以编码时空局部几何（包括全部曲率信息），且该编码具有可控的、可证的连续极限行为，为因果集量子引力提供了关键的半经典一致性支撑。

Abstract: A causal set is a locally finite partially ordered set. Causal set theory proposes such discrete structures as a basis for spacetime physics. Connecting this description with continuum physics requires recovering the geometric quantities used in general relativity and controlling the errors introduced by discreteness. Here, with dimension and sampling density specified, we construct approximate normal coordinates, estimate parallel transport, and recover the full Riemann tensor from causal order and element counts. For samples embedded in a fixed smooth spacetime and satisfying stated uniform counting assumptions, we prove a uniform curvature error bound that tends to zero as the sampling density increases.

</details>


### [111] [Improved constraints on higher-derivative effective field theory extensions of general relativity with GW230627_015337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36728)
*Tao Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA引力波数据，通过SEOBNRv5HM_ROM波形模型引入三种高阶曲率EFT修正（5PN、6PN、8PN），对92个双黑洞事件进行Fisher矩阵分析，并对最强约束事件GW230627_015337开展贝叶斯分析，结果均支持广义相对论，给出特征长度尺度的90%单边上限为11.1–18.3 km。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验广义相对论在强场引力下的有效性，特别是通过有效场论中高阶曲率修正（立方与四次曲率项）探测可能的新物理。

Method: 将三种EFT高曲率耦合（两个偶宇称立方项、一个四次项）以理论无关方式嵌入SEOBNRv5HM_ROM波形的各多极矩中，完整包含高阶模式与IMR信号；结合Fisher矩阵预估与全贝叶斯后验采样，并采用log Bayes factor进行模型比较。

Result: 所有三个EFT耦合在90%置信度下与零一致；对应特征长度尺度的90%单边上限分别为17.1 km、11.1 km和18.3 km；log Bayes因子介于−5.49至−2.51之间，显著支持GR；约束精度达10–20 km量级，数值上优于典型先前的旋进与铃宕单独分析。

Conclusion: 当前引力波观测在10–20 km特征尺度上未发现偏离广义相对论的证据，对高阶曲率EFT修正施加了迄今最紧致的IMR联合约束之一。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) observations of binary black holes provide a powerful probe of general relativity (GR) in the strong-field regime. In effective field theory (EFT) extensions of GR, higher-curvature operators modify the binary dynamics and introduce corrections to the inspiral phase. In this work, we consider two parity-even cubic-curvature couplings and one quartic-curvature coupling, whose leading phase corrections enter at 5PN, 6PN, and 8PN order, respectively. We incorporate these corrections into the individual multipoles of the \texttt{SEOBNRv5HM\_ROM} waveform using the flexible theory-independent framework, allowing higher-order modes and the full inspiral-merger-ringdown signal to be used in the analysis. We first perform a Fisher-matrix analysis of 92 binary-black-hole events selected from a parent sample of 273 events in the public LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA catalogs. GW230627\_015337 provides the tightest forecast constraints on all three EFT couplings and is therefore selected for a full Bayesian analysis. The posterior distributions are consistent with GR, with all three couplings consistent with zero at 90\% credibility. The corresponding one-sided 90\% upper limits on the characteristic length scales are \(17.1\), \(11.1\), and \(18.3\,\mathrm{km}\). The log Bayes factors of the three EFT models relative to GR range from \(-5.49\) to \(-2.51\), favoring GR under the adopted priors. These bounds probe higher-curvature length scales at the level of \(10\)--\(20\,\mathrm{km}\) and are numerically tighter than representative previous inspiral and ringdown constraints, although differences in the models and analysis assumptions should be taken into account.

</details>


### [112] [Impact of deformation parameters on the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansions of Schwarzschild-AdS black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36730)
*Faisal Javed,A. Eid,Arfa Waseem,Jiafeng Guo,Abdelmalek Bouzenada,Sulaman Shaukat,Farruh Atamurotov,N. Mustapha*

TL;DR: 本文研究了变形Schwarzschild-AdS黑洞的灰体因子与焦耳-汤姆孙膨胀行为：通过曲率坐标将Klein-Gordon方程化为Schrödinger型波方程，获得精确有效势；发现形变参数增强有效势，显著影响无质量标量场的吸收/散射；高频波更易穿透势垒，低频波则反射；热力学方面，利用等焓曲线识别加热/冷却区，并揭示形变参数与黑洞质量对等焓行为的增强效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子场在变形AdS黑洞背景下的散射特性（灰体因子）及其热力学相变行为（Joule-Thomson效应），以探索量子引力修正对黑洞信息、辐射谱和热力学稳定性的影响。

Method: 1) 采用tortoise坐标将Klein-Gordon方程约化为一维Schrödinger型波方程；2) 解析推导并分析有效势V_eff(r*)关于形变参数α、质量M及角动量ℓ的依赖性；3) 数值/半解析研究波函数在近事件视界区域的反射/透射行为；4) 基于黑洞热力学第一定律与状态方程，计算等焓曲线并分析Joule-Thomson系数μ_JT = (∂T/∂P)_H。

Result: 1) 形变参数α增大导致V_eff整体抬升，尤其在视界附近显著增强势垒高度；2) α ≠ 0时Schwarzschild极限下V_eff远高于标准情形；3) 高频标量波透射率上升，低频波反射主导；4) 等焓曲线向高压/高温方向扩展，μ_JT零点（反转温度）随α与M增大而升高，冷却区扩大。

Conclusion: 形变参数作为量子引力或高维效应的有效低能表征，不仅调控黑洞量子辐射谱（通过灰体因子），还深刻改变其热力学响应特性（Joule-Thomson行为），表明此类变形黑洞具有更丰富的散射动力学与非平凡热力学相结构。

Abstract: This paper investigates the greybody factor and Joule-Thomson expansion of deformed Schwarzschild-AdS black hole models. We constructed the Klein-Gordon equation into a Schrödinger-like wave equation using tortoise coordinates and derived the exact effective potential. Also, we analyzed its behavior under varying physical parameters, including deformation parameters, mass and angular momentum. In this case, our results illustrate that increasing the deformation parameter enhances the effective potential, significantly impacting the absorption and scattering of massless scalar fields. It is noted that the Schwarzschild black hole effective potential is greatly increased when the deformation parameter is present. Near the event horizon, we found that waves behave differently, with higher-frequency waves more easily penetrating potential obstacles and lower-frequency waves bouncing off them. Furthermore, we investigated the thermodynamic characteristics of black holes using the Joule-Thomson expansion, detecting isenthalpic curves that indicate heating and cooling zones controlled by pressure and temperature changes. In this context, we tested the influence of deformation black hole parameters; the isenthalpic curves are enhanced by increasing the deformation parameters and the black hole mass.

</details>


### [113] [Novel topological subclasses of a magnetic black hole in causal nonlinear electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36796)
*Qi-Qi Liang,De-jiang Yin,Yu-Die Wan,Zheng-Wen Long*
