TL;DR: 本文通过将黑洞态视为热力学参数空间中的拓扑缺陷，结合广义离壳自由能与Duan的φ-映射拓扑流理论，研究了爱因斯坦引力耦合因果非线性电动力学下的静态磁黑洞。在正则系综中，识别出四类缺陷曲线构型，包括已知的W^{0+↔1−}类、\overline{W}^{1−}子类，以及新提出的\overline{W}^{0+↔1−}子类——其特征为成对的生成/湮灭点及不稳定的最内与最外分支；随着辅助反温度升高，系统经历从两态→三态→单态的演化，伴随一个不稳定小黑洞缺陷穿越物理下边界，使全局拓扑数由W=0跃变为W=−1，标志一次热力学拓扑相变。结果强调物理视界边界在热力学拓扑分类中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示黑洞热力学相变的拓扑本质，超越传统基于自由能极值或比热发散的判据，建立以参数空间缺陷结构和拓扑数变化为核心的分类框架。

Method: 采用Duan的φ-映射拓扑流理论构造热力学参数空间中的缺陷流J^μ = δ²(φ)∂_μφ¹∂_νφ²ε^{μν}，结合广义离壳自由能F(β, M)定义缺陷源密度ρ = ε^{ab}∂_a n^1 ∂_b n^2 δ²(n)，其中n^i = ∂_i F / |∇F|为归一化梯度场；在单视界物理扇区内分析缺陷曲线（即n^i=0的解集）的分岔、生成/湮灭及拓扑荷W = ∫ J⁰ d²λ演化。

Result: 发现新拓扑子类\\overline{W}^{0+↔1−}，其缺陷曲线含一对生成/湮灭点且内外分支均不稳定；确认边界诱导的W: 0 → −1跃变对应热力学拓扑相变；证明相同反温度端点行为与小→大黑洞绕数序列可对应不同缺陷演化路径。

Conclusion: 物理视界边界是决定热力学拓扑分类的关键约束；因果非线性电动力学黑洞族为探索更丰富缺陷结构（如高阶分岔、链状缺陷）及新型热力学拓扑相变（如W数多重跃迁、缺陷网络重组）提供了可控模型。

Abstract: By treating black-hole states as topological defects in thermodynamic parameter space, we investigate a static, asymptotically flat magnetic black holes in Einstein gravity coupled to causal nonlinear electrodynamics. Using the generalized off-shell free energy and Duan's $\phi$-mapping topological current theory, we analyze the physical single-horizon sector in the canonical ensemble. We identify four representative defect-curve profiles, including realizations of the conjectured $W^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$ class and $\overline{W}^{1-}$ subclass, as well as a new thermodynamic topological subclass, $\overline{W}^{0+\leftrightarrow1-}$. This new subclass features paired generation and annihilation points, with unstable innermost and outermost branches. As the auxiliary inverse temperature increases, the system evolves from two equilibrium states to three and then back to one, with an unstable small-black-hole defect entering through the physical lower boundary and changing the global topological number from $W=0$ to $W=-1$. This boundary-induced change signals a thermodynamic topological phase transition. These results show that the same inverse-temperature endpoint behavior and winding-number sequence from small to large black holes can accompany different defect evolution, highlighting the role of the physical horizon boundary in thermodynamic topological classification. More broadly, this causal nonlinear-electrodynamics black-hole family provides a setting for discovering further defect structures and thermodynamic topological phase transitions.

</details>


### [114] [Realistic neutron stars in the constant-Q branch of f(Q) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36814)
*Debadri Bhattacharjee,Pradip Kumar Chattopadhyay,P. H. R. S. Moraes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在对称仿射平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）框架下，研究了中子星的流体静力学平衡与全局结构，采用常数非度规性标量Q分支的f(Q)引力模型，并提出三种新型非线性f(Q)理论；结合现代真实物态方程（GPPVA(DD2)和SLy4），数值求解修正TOV方程，并用HZN准则检验稳定性；结果表明Born-Infeld与Hu-Sawicki类模型能良好复原广义相对论极限并符合低质量中子星半径观测，但最大质量受限；而Tsujikawa类模型显著增强有效引力耦合，可支持~3.36 M⊙中子星，为GW190814次级天体提供可能解释，但高估典型中子星半径；二者在最大质量–半径约束间呈现现象学互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非度规性引力（f(Q) gravity）对中子星结构的影响，特别是检验其是否能在不违背多信使观测（如GW170817、GW190814）的前提下，同时解释致密星体的最大质量和半径特征，从而约束非线性非度规性理论的参数空间。

Method: 在metric-affine几何框架下，选取正则的常数非度规性标量Q = Q_*分支，构建三种非线性f(Q)模型（Born-Infeld型、Hu-Sawicki类比型、Tsujikawa启发型）；耦合GPPVA(DD2)与SLy4统一物态方程，数值求解f(Q)修正的Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff方程；通过Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov（HZN）稳定性判据分析质量-半径关系。

Result: Born-Infeld与Hu-Sawicki类模型在控制参数大时趋近GR极限，低质量中子星（M < 2.0 M⊙）半径与X射线双星及GW170817一致，但最大质量上限分别为2.43 M⊙（DD2）和2.06 M⊙（SLy4）；Tsujikawa模型则大幅提高有效引力强度，支持高达~3.36 M⊙的中子星，可自然容纳GW190814中2.59^{+0.08}_{−0.09} M⊙次级致密天体，但导致典型中子星半径系统性偏大。

Conclusion: 在常数Q分支的f(Q)引力中，最大质量与半径约束存在本质的 phenomenological complementarity；多信使天文观测（引力波、X射线脉冲星计时、NICER等）可协同限制非线性非度规性函数的形式与参数，凸显对称仿射平行引力作为替代理论的可观测区分能力。

Abstract: We investigate the hydrostatic equilibrium and global structure of neutron stars within symmetric teleparallel gravity, where gravitational interactions are mediated by the non-metricity scalar $Q$. Working within the metric-affine formulation, we adopt the regular, constant-$Q$ branch ($Q = Q_*$) of $f(Q)$ gravity, which preserves regularity at the stellar centre while maintaining explicit dependence on the non-linear functional form through $f(Q_*)$ and its derivatives. We propose three novel non-linear $f(Q)$ extensions: (i) a Born-Infeld-inspired model, (ii) a Hu--Sawicki analogue, and (iii) a Tsujikawa-inspired model. Incorporating state-of-the-art realistic unified equations of state, namely GPPVA(DD2) and SLy4, we numerically solve the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations and evaluate stellar stability via the Harrison-Zel'dovich-Novikov criterion. The Born-Infeld and Hu-Sawicki analogues smoothly recover the general relativistic limit as their control parameters increase, predicting stellar radii for low-mass objects ($M < 2.0\,M_\odot$) that match observational constraints from X-ray binaries and GW170817; however, their maximum masses remain capped below $2.43\,M_\odot$ (DD2) and $2.06\,M_\odot$ (SLy4). Conversely, the Tsujikawa-inspired model significantly stiffens the effective gravitational coupling, accommodating massive compact objects up to $\sim 3.36\,M_\odot$ and offering a viable physical origin for the $2.59_{-0.09}^{+0.08}\,M_\odot$ secondary object in GW190814, albeit at the expense of overpredicting radii for typical low-mass neutron stars. These contrasting behaviours demonstrate a key phenomenological complementarity between maximum mass and radius bounds within the constant-$Q$ framework, highlighting the utility of multimessenger observations in constraining non-linear non-metricity gravity.

</details>


### [115] [Primordial black holes from a type-IIB/F theory inflaton potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36863)
*Chitrak Sarkar,Amitava Choudhuri,Buddhadeb Ghosh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于IIB型弦理论大体积场景的含Kähler模修正的暴胀势，通过引入局域高斯隆起实现超慢滚暴胀，从而在k~1.12×10⁹ Mpc⁻¹处显著增强曲率扰动功率谱，生成质量为10⁻⁶M⊙的原初黑洞（PBHs），其丰度满足当前微引力透镜限制；所预言的标量诱导引力波处于SKA探测灵敏度范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释暗物质候选体（如PBHs）的起源，并弥合弦论紧致化模型与可观测宇宙学信号（如PBH丰度、引力波）之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在type-IIB/F弦论大体积场景中，利用微扰与非微扰修正稳定全部Kähler模，构造暴胀势；引入单个Kähler模的小高斯扰动形成局域势隆起，诱发超慢滚；数值求解Mukhanov-Sasaki方程得到曲率功率谱；采用Press-Schechter形式计算PBH丰度f_PBH，并结合δ_c=0.439与微透镜观测约束；计算对应PBHs的Hawking温度与蒸发时间。

Result: 曲率功率谱在k≈1.12×10⁹ Mpc⁻¹处获得可持续增强；生成质量为10⁻⁶M⊙的PBHs，f_PBH满足当前微透镜上限；Hawking温度T_H≈2.78×10⁻¹⁵ GeV，蒸发时间τ≈8.5×10³⁸ Gyr；标量诱导引力波信号落入SKA探测窗口。

Conclusion: 该弦论启发的暴胀模型可自然产生符合观测约束的PBH暗物质候选体，并提供可检验的引力波预言，强化了弦宇宙学与未来21cm/引力波天文学的联系。

Abstract: We study the possibility of the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs) using an inflaton potential constructed by stabilizing all Kähler moduli employing both the perturbative and non-perturbative corrections in the large volume scenario in type-IIB/F string theory. We further introduce a small perturbation in one of the Kähler moduli that modifies the existing potential in the field space. The effective correction in the moduli space is made by introducing a localised Gaussian bump, which induces an ultra-slow-roll feature in the inflaton potential during the early cosmological inflation. The evolution of the amplitude of the curvature power spectrum is solved numerically by using the Mukhanov-Sasaki equation, with Bunch-Davies initial conditions. The inclusion of the Gaussian bump produces a sustainable enhancement in the amplitude of curvature power spectra around $k\sim 1.12 \times 10^{9} Mpc^{-1}$ for the formation of PBHs. The abundance of PBHs ($f_{PBH}$) is calculated using the Press-Schechter formalism for different values of critical density contrast and presented in the background of contemporary $f_{PBH}$ bounds and checked for viability with existing microlensing bounds, corresponding to a mass value $10^{-6}M_\odot$ for $\delta$ threshold value $\delta_c=0.439$. The Hawking temperature and evaporation time are also determined for these black holes and found to be of the order of $2.78\times 10^{-15}$ GeV and $8.5\times10^{38}$ Gyr, respectively. The enhancement of curvature power spectra lies in the sensitivity region of the SKA observations, suggesting an indication that scalar-induced gravity waves may be probed in future observations.

</details>


### [116] [Self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson Solitons in Curved Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36923)
*Kei-ichi Maeda*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了弯曲时空下自引力Schrödinger–Poisson（SP）孤子的局域惯性系描述，将背景时空效应通过由曲率决定的有效潮汐势引入SP方程；应用于Kerr黑洞赤道圆轨道上的孤子，发现潮汐场诱导其形变并产生质量四极矩，由此定义了潮汐形变率与无量纲Love数；进而估算旋转形变孤子的引力辐射特征应变，并与未来空间引力波探测器灵敏度曲线比较，指出可观测参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自引力玻色子孤子在强引力场（如黑洞附近）中的潮汐响应及其可能的引力波信号，为暗物质候选者（如超轻玻色子）提供可观测的天体物理探针。

Method: 在弱场、非相对论近似下，采用局域惯性系方法将弯曲时空效应编码为有效潮汐势；求解修正后的SP方程以获得孤子形变；定义潮汐形变率和无量纲Love数表征线性响应；基于四极矩公式计算引力波特征应变。

Result: 推导出Kerr黑洞圆轨道上SP孤子的潮汐诱导四极矩；给出其Love数与形变率的解析/半解析表达式；估算的特征应变在特定孤子质量（~10^{-19}–10^{-17} eV）与轨道半径（几倍至数十倍史瓦西半径）下可进入LISA或μAres等空间探测器的灵敏度范围。

Conclusion: 该框架为系统研究黑洞时空中自引力玻色子孤子的潮汐响应及引力波辐射提供了理论基础，表明此类天体物理对象可能成为未来空间引力波天文台的潜在目标。

Abstract: We formulate a description of self-gravitating Schrödinger--Poisson (SP) solitons in curved spacetime using a local inertial frame centered on the soliton. In the weak-field and non-relativistic regime, the effect of the background spacetime is incorporated into the SP equations through an effective tidal potential determined by the spacetime curvature. We apply this framework to a soliton moving along a circular equatorial geodesic around a Kerr black hole. The tidal field contains stationary and time-dependent quadrupolar components, which induce a deformation of the soliton and an associated mass quadrupole moment. We formulate the quadrupolar response in terms of the tidal deformability and the dimensionless Love number. We then estimate the gravitational radiation associated with the rotating deformed soliton and evaluate the corresponding characteristic strain for representative soliton sizes and orbital radii. A comparison with representative sensitivity curves of planned space-based gravitational-wave detectors identifies parameter regimes in which the estimated characteristic strain may lie within their sensitivity ranges. Our framework provides a systematic basis for investigating the tidal response of self-gravitating bosonic solitons in black-hole spacetimes and their potential gravitational-wave signatures.

</details>


### [117] [Can One Hear the Absence of a Singularity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37028)
*Matteo Della Rocca,Paolo Pani,Andrea P. Sanna*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则黑洞（即无曲率奇点的黑洞）的拟正则模谱在高阶模极限下的行为，发现其与史瓦西黑洞存在本质差异：正则黑洞的拟正则频率实部渐近趋于零，而非非零常数，表明奇点消除在谱上留下不可抹除的印记；该现象被解释为黑洞谱不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力预期能解决广义相对论预言的曲率奇点，产生内部正则的黑洞几何；需探究此类正则化对可观测量（如拟正则模）的影响，尤其是其是否在小正则化参数下仍可区分。

Method: 采用系统化的单值性（monodromy）方法进行解析分析，并辅以数值计算验证；聚焦于大阶数（large-overtone）极限下的拟正则频率渐近行为。

Result: 对于一大类正则黑洞（含多个已知解），其拟正则模实部在大阶数极限下渐近趋于零，而虚部呈线性增长；该行为与史瓦西黑洞（实部趋于非零常数）不连续，且不随正则化参数趋于零而恢复。

Conclusion: 奇点的物理消除在黑洞拟正则谱的大阶数极限中留下普适、非微扰的印记；该谱不稳定性表明，即使微小的量子引力修正也会导致拟正则谱发生本质改变，因而该极限下的谱可作为探测奇点分辨率的敏感探针。

Abstract: One of the central expectations of quantum gravity is the resolution of the curvature singularities predicted by General Relativity, potentially giving rise to effective black-hole geometries that are regular inside the horizon. We show that, for a very broad class of regular black holes including several known solutions, the quasinormal-mode spectrum in the large-overtone limit differs discontinuously from that of a Schwarzschild black hole, even when the regularization parameter is arbitrarily small. The real part of the quasinormal frequencies vanishes asymptotically, rather than approaching a nonzero constant, as generically occurs in General Relativity and other theories predicting black holes with curvature singularities. The asymptotic black-hole spectrum thus retains a distinctive imprint of singularity resolution. We establish these results analytically through a systematic formulation of the monodromy method and confirm them numerically. We interpret this phenomenon as a manifestation of black-hole spectral instability, whereby arbitrarily small deviations from vacuum General Relativity produce nonperturbative changes in the large-overtone spectrum.

</details>


### [118] [Genesis in Minimally Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37063)
*Alexander Ganz,Paul Martens,Shinji Mukohyama,Pavel Petrov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在II型最小修正引力框架下构建了一个Genesis宇宙学模型，背景解在过去测地完备且渐近趋于闵可夫斯基时空；物质满足零能条件，标量扰动无鬼且无紫外梯度不稳定性，但红外区域存在有限时段的负声速平方；数值模拟显示标量谱近似尺度不变且略红倾，张量谱蓝倾且张量-标量比被强烈压低；物质场涨落δχ渐近归一化正则，背景依赖的三阶耦合在远古时期（固定非零动量）消失，剩余的物质-引力子及引力子自相互作用均为普朗克压制。


<details>

Method: 基于type-II最小修正引力理论，求解过去渐近Minkowski的背景解，分析线性标量与张量扰动的动力学（包括声速、稳定性、功率谱），并数值演化超视界模式以获取功率谱形状；通过选择正则物质涨落δχ分析高阶相互作用的行为。

Result: 获得过去测地完备、零能条件满足的Genesis背景；标量扰动ghost-free且无UV gradient instability，但存在有限IR负c_R²区间；数值给出近尺度不变、微红倾的标量谱和蓝倾、极小r的张量谱；δχ作为正则变量使背景依赖三阶耦合在远古消失，剩余相互作用皆为M_Pl^{-1}压制。

Conclusion: type-II最小修正引力可自洽实现无奇点Genesis宇宙学，其线性扰动谱与观测兼容，非线性相互作用在远古自然压低，为替代暴胀提供有力候选。

Abstract: We construct a Genesis scenario within the framework of a type-II minimally modified gravity. The background solution is past geodesically complete and approaches Minkowski spacetime in the asymptotic past. While the Hubble expansion rate grows, the matter sector satisfies the null-energy condition. Scalar perturbations are ghost-free and have no ultraviolet gradient instability, although the squared sound speed $c_\mathcal{R}^2$ becomes negative within a finite infrared region in the Fourier space for a finite time interval. Numerical evolution, including superhorizon growth, yields a nearly scale-invariant, slightly red-tilted scalar spectrum over a broad region of parameter space. The tensor spectrum is blue, with a strongly suppressed tensor-to-scalar ratio. Finally, using the matter fluctuation $\delta\chi$, whose asymptotic normalization is regular, we find that the background-dependent cubic couplings vanish in the far past at fixed nonzero momenta. The surviving matter--graviton and graviton self-interactions are Planck suppressed.

</details>

Conclusion: 8形/C形热力学相结构是正则AdS黑洞的普适特征，其根源在于正则性条件M₁=q³/α对热力学相空间的非平庸重构，而非球对称性假设；该结构在引入角动量后依然稳健，证实其几何起源在于能量密度的有界性与中心正则性。

Abstract: We use the modified Newman--Janis algorithm to obtain the rotating ``mother'' black hole solution of the rotating Hayward-AdS black hole. We further derive the fundamental thermodynamic quantities of the ``mother'' black hole in the full unconstrained phase space $(S,J,q,\alpha,P)$, and impose the regularity condition $M_1=q^3/\alpha$ to obtain the thermodynamic quantities of the rotating Hayward-AdS regular black hole. This order of operations gives a Hawking temperature identical to the surface gravity temperature and produces the correct response functions of the regular black hole. For $P<P_c$, the Hawking temperature $T_2$ possesses a local maximum and a local minimum, which divides the black hole into small, intermediate, and large black hole branches, and the $P$--$V$ isotherms display oscillatory behavior characteristic of a liquid/gas phase transition. The small and large black hole branches are locally stable, whereas the intermediate black hole branch has negative heat capacity and is locally unstable. Increasing the rotation parameter $a$, magnetic charge $q$, or pressure $P$ reduces the region of the intermediate branch. The $G_2$--$T_2$ diagram shows that the phase structure, in contrast to the standard swallowtail of the Reissner--Nordström-AdS and Kerr--Newman-AdS black holes, is 8-shaped or C-shaped. These two shapes are divided by the characteristic pressure $P_z$. For $P<P_z$, the curve is 8-shaped, corresponding to a first-order small/large black hole transition. For $P_z<P<P_c$, the curve is C-shaped, corresponding to a zeroth-order transition. These results show that the 8-shaped/C-shaped phase structure characteristic of regular AdS black holes persists under rotation, indicating that it stems from the restructuring of the thermodynamic phase space by the regularity condition rather than from spherical symmetry.

</details>

Conclusion: 该BF-耦合多形式标量模型在保持动力学良定义（正动能、光速传播）前提下，能实现多样化加速行为；标量势形态由Noether对称性严格限定为双曲平方或指数型；BF拓扑项是动力学调节器而非暗能量源；形式场协同作用可生成区别于纯标量驱动的晚期标度律。

Abstract: We study a canonical scalar field coupled to isotropic triplets of Abelian one-forms and two-forms through scalar-dependent kinetic functions and a constant topological $BF$ interaction. By retaining the lapse in the homogeneous reduction, we recover the Friedmann constraint and identify a sufficient domain in which all twelve physical modes have positive kinetic terms and luminal high-frequency propagation. A separate exponential model admits an accelerating mixed solution with $a\propto t^2$, nonzero asymptotic form fractions, and bounded normalized linear perturbations. For the Noether analysis, we choose a quadratic effective potential in flat minisuperspace coordinates and solve the zero-boundary determining equations. The canonical scalar potential is hyperbolic-squared or exponential, rather than a power law in the scalar field. The causal character of the potential coordinate separates three branches: Branch I admits a de Sitter limit, Branch II cannot sustain acceleration on regular future-complete expanding trajectories, and Branch III gives an exact solution with both form sectors active and late expansion $a\propto t^{4/3}$. Comparisons with consistent uncoupled and reduced-field limits separate the form contributions from the scalar-driven acceleration. The $BF$ term changes the finite-time exchange between the form sectors but supplies no direct negative-pressure source.

</details>


### [121] [Geodesic dynamics and tidal forces in a quantum-regularized black hole from string T-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37331)

Result: 1) ℓ₀与Q共同决定几何相变：当Q² < Q²_crit(ℓ₀)时存在双视界黑洞，Q² > Q²_crit时为无视界正则几何；2) 所有潮汐分量在r→0处有限，出现r< r_comp区域满足∂_r(R^t_{rtr})<0 ∧ ∂_r(R^θ_{rθr})<0（双向压缩）；3) 增大ℓ₀或Q均抑制累积径向拉伸∫|ξ^r|dτ，而∫|ξ^θ|dτ对Q几乎不敏感；4) ℓ₀→0极限下严格恢复Reissner–Nordström解及其奇点发散行为。

Conclusion: T-对偶正则化不仅消除了经典奇点处的潮汐发散，还催生出新颖的双向压缩动力学区域；电荷Q主要调制潮汐特征的空间定位与强度，而ℓ₀主导整体正则性与强场稳定性；该框架为检验量子引力效应对致密天体观测信号（如吸积盘谱线展宽、引力波尾波）的影响提供了可控模型。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the geodesic dynamics and tidal force effects in neutral and electrically charged regular spacetimes inspired by T-duality. We first analyze the geometric properties of these solutions, including their regular central structure and horizon configurations, and show how the interplay between the T-duality scale $\ell_0$ and the electric charge $Q$ determines the transition between black hole and horizonless geometries. We then study the motion of massive and massless particles through the corresponding effective potentials, examining the existence and stability of circular orbits and the behavior of their characteristic radii. We further investigate the tidal forces experienced by observers in radial free fall, focusing on the behavior of the radial and angular components, their extrema and sign changes, and the location of these characteristic points relative to the horizon structure of the geometry. We show that the T-duality regularization renders the tidal forces finite in the central region and gives rise to different stretching and compression regimes, including a region of simultaneous compression in both the radial and angular directions, while the electric charge modifies the strength and location of these tidal features. Finally, we analyze the evolution of the geodesic deviation vector for two sets of initial conditions. We find that both the T-duality scale and the electric charge tend to suppress the accumulated radial stretching, whereas the angular deformation is considerably less sensitive to the charge. These results show how the quantum regularization induced by T-duality and the presence of electric charge modify the orbital dynamics and tidal response of these geometries in the strong-field regime. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the corresponding classical limits are recovered appropriately.

</details>


### [122] [Quasinormal Ringing of a Schwarzschild Black Hole Surrounded by a King Dark Matter Halo: Higher-Order WKB Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37501)
*Semra Gurtas Dogan,Hassan Hassanabadi,Abdullah Guvendi*

Main category: gr-qc

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕对黑洞准正规模的影响，以期通过引力波观测约束暗物质分布模型，特别是King型晕在强引力场中的可观测效应。

Result: 暗物质晕导致QNMs实部（振荡频率）与虚部（阻尼率）均减小；实部变化主要由光子球内暗物质质量决定；虚部变化与零测地线Lyapunov指数/角频率比值成正比；外置晕情形下频率按引力红移缩放；密度跃变引起最大约9%的阻尼率修正；连续分布下对红移关系的偏离≤1.5%（r_in=3M）或≤0.5%（r_in≥4M）；所有检验参数空间内无不稳定模。

Conclusion: King型暗物质晕对Schwarzschild黑洞准正规模有系统性、可建模的修正效应，其特征依赖于晕质量的空间分布（尤其是否穿透视界）及界面性质（连续或跃变）；该结果为利用未来高信噪比引力波探测区分暗物质模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study scalar and electromagnetic perturbations of a Schwarzschild black hole (BH) surrounded by a dark matter halo described by the King density profile. The lapse function can be expressed as $1-2[M+M_{\rm DM}(r)]/r$, where $M_{\rm DM}(r)$ is the halo mass enclosed within radius $r$; thus, the halo affects the geometry through its enclosed-mass function. We compute the quasinormal frequencies using the sixth-order WKB method and its Padé-improved version. Rewriting the lapse function in terms of the inverse hyperbolic sine avoids the severe numerical cancellations that arise when evaluating its high-order derivatives. For the configurations examined, the fundamental frequencies agree with those extracted from time-domain evolution to within $0.2\%$.The halo lowers both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate. The change of the real part is almost entirely accounted for by the halo mass enclosed by the photon sphere, and the additional decrease of the damping rate follows the ratio of the Lyapunov exponent to the angular frequency of the circular null orbit. Since part of the halo lies inside the horizon in this metric, we also place the halo outside the BH by means of an Einstein cluster. The frequencies then decrease by a similar amount, but the real part follows the Schwarzschild value multiplied by the gravitational redshift factor of the halo. A jump in the density at the inner edge of the halo scatters the waves and changes the damping rate by up to about $9\%$. For a continuous density profile, deviations from the redshift relation remain below about $1.5\%$ for $r_{\rm in}=3M$ and below about $0.5\%$ for the cases with $r_{\rm in}\geq4M$. No unstable modes are found within the perturbation sectors and parameter configurations examined.

</details>


### [123] [One Method to Find Them All: Analytic Black Holes beyond Einstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37515)
*Dongjun Li,Jierui Hu,Nicolás Yunes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个统一框架，用于生成Kerr黑洞在高曲率修正下的稳态轴对称形变解，首次在任意亚极端自旋下得到六个高曲率相互作用的闭式解析解，无需慢旋转近似、数值拟合或模分解，并揭示手征性主导解的解析结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于潮汐曲率与物理源的统一形变框架，采用稳态轴对称假设，在耦合常数领头阶展开下直接求解修正爱因斯坦方程，避免慢旋转近似、数值拟合与球谐/连带勒让德函数模分解。

Result: 获得六个独立高曲率项（含手征性区分）在任意亚极端自旋参数a<M下的闭式度规解；所有解严格满足场方程，且与已有模分解结果及视界热力学量解析预言一致。

Conclusion: 手征性是组织高曲率修正下Kerr形变解析结构的核心对称性，该框架可推广至一大类纯度规高曲率引力理论，并为强场检验（如LIGO/Virgo、EHT、XRISM）提供普适几何基础。

Abstract: Rotating black holes offer powerful probes of gravity, but constructing their geometries beyond vacuum general relativity remains challenging. Here we develop a unified framework for constructing stationary, axisymmetric deformations of Kerr from their tidal curvature and physical sources. As a demonstration, we obtain closed-form solutions for six higher-curvature interactions at leading order in the couplings and for generic subextremal spin, without slow-rotation expansions, numerical fitting, or mode decomposition. The solutions pass stringent checks against the field equations, previous mode-by-mode constructions, and known analytic predictions for horizon quantities. We find that the chirality of the interactions organizes the analytic structure of these solutions, a pattern that likely extends to a large class of purely metric higher-curvature theories. Our framework opens a systematic route to black-hole geometries in beyond-Einstein theories and perturbative astrophysical environments. These geometries provide a foundation for strong-field tests with gravitational waves and other observational channels, such as black-hole images and X-ray spectroscopy, as well as theoretical studies of near-extremal black holes.

</details>


### [124] [Gravitational wave signatures of periodic orbits around a Schwarzschild-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37525)
*Mirzabek Alloqulov,Sanjar Shaymatov,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了新改进的Schwarzschild黑洞背景下，偏离标准Schwarzschild几何对类时测地线周期轨道及相应引力波辐射的影响；通过径向与方位角频率的共振条件确定周期轨道，利用STF质量四极矩方法计算引力波偏振，并分析参数ξ和γ对轨道构型与波形的可观测修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验非标准黑洞模型（改进Schwarzschild解）的动力学可观测效应，特别是其对周期轨道结构与引力波信号的修正，以探索强场引力检验的新途径。

Method: 基于改进Schwarzschild度规求解类时测地线；采用轨道频率比（Ω_r/Ω_φ）的有理数判据识别周期轨道；数值积分重构粒子运动；应用对称无迹（STF）质量四极矩公式计算h_+和h_×极化分量。

Conclusion: 该改进黑洞模型的动力学特征可通过周期轨道引力波模板进行参数化探测，ξ和γ为可被LISA或第三代地面探测器约束的有效形变参数。

Abstract: We investigate how deviations from the Schwarzschild geometry affect the motion of massive test particles and the gravitational radiation associated with their periodic trajectories around a new improved Schwarzschild black hole. Periodic configurations are determined by examining the commensurability of the radial and azimuthal orbital frequencies. For representative trajectories, we numerically reconstruct the particle motion and calculate corresponding gravitational wave polarizations using the symmetric trace-free mass-quadrupole approach. By comparing these signals with their Schwarzschild counterparts, we assess how the modified spacetime geometry is encoded in the radiation. Our analysis shows that the parameters $\xi$ and $\gamma$ produce modest changes in the orbital configurations and their associated waveforms, providing a means of investigating the dynamical signatures of this BH model.

</details>

Method: 将超轻暗物质的波函数动力学（含波依赖吸积率与修正的动力学摩擦力）耦合进相对论性EMRI波形建模，在LISA灵敏度下对模拟信号进行贝叶斯联合参数推断。

Conclusion: 超轻暗物质在致密尖峰中的波-粒子二象性可在EMRI引力波信号中留下可观测印记，使LISA成为首个具备直接测定暗物质粒子质量潜力的空间引力波天文台。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that the mass of dark matter particles cannot be determined from their gravitational effects when dark matter behaves as collisionless matter. Here we show that extreme-mass-ratio inspirals observable by millihertz gravitational-wave observatories such as the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) could measure the mass of ultralight dark matter particles in sufficiently dense dark matter spikes. The separation of scales between the two black holes allows ultralight dark matter to form a collisionless density spike around the massive primary while retaining wave effects in its interaction with the stellar-mass companion. We incorporate wave-dependent accretion and dynamical friction into relativistic inspiral waveforms and jointly infer the dark matter particle mass, spike normalisation and binary parameters from simulated LISA signals. For massive black holes at the low-mass end of the LISA population ($M\sim 10^5 M_\odot$), our forecasts show that particle masses spanning $10^{-14}-10^{-11}\,\mathrm{eV}$ could be measured to within an order of magnitude.

</details>


### [126] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了广义Dirac方程在对称度规平行引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）弱场背景下的非相对论极限，通过Foldy–Wouthuysen展开至1/m²阶，导出了包含新型自旋-引力耦合项（如直接自旋-引力、各向异性自旋-动量-引力、潮汐自旋-动量耦合）的有效哈密顿量，并给出了地球弱场下电子的量级估计以支持截断合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称度规平行引力框架下费米子自旋与非度规性（non-metricity）之间的新耦合机制，拓展传统Dirac理论在修改引力中的低能唯象基础。

Method: 在重合标架（coincident gauge）和各向同性坐标下的弱场Schwarzschild几何中，构造含完整Clifford代数基的广义旋量联络，推导广义Dirac哈密顿量，并实施逐阶Foldy–Wouthuysen变换至O(1/m²)，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项。

Result: 获得块对角化哈密顿量，除标准引力类比项（动能、自旋-轨道、Darwin项）外，新增三项：直接自旋-引力耦合 ∝ S·∇Φ、各向异性自旋-动量-引力耦合 ∝ S_i p_j ∂_i ∂_j Φ、潮汐自旋-动量耦合 ∝ S_i p_j ∂_i ∂_j Φ（具张量结构差异）；所有新项源于广义旋量联络对非度规性的依赖。

Conclusion: 广义Dirac方程在对称度规平行引力背景中自然诱导出超越标准广义相对论与自旋耦合的新低能相互作用通道，其有效哈密顿量为实验探测非度规性—费米子自旋相互作用提供了可检验的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>

### [127] [Stability analysis of the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$ describing polytropic gas spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35932)
*S. C. Mancas,Ronald Adams*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了临界多方指数 n=5 的Lane–Emden方程，揭示其解的全局结构：Schuster 解作为中心正则解和分界解，具有唯一性与稳定性分岔特性；通过Hamilton化、Weierstrass ℘-函数显式求解、谱分析（含负本征值与阈值共振）及非线性不稳定性证明，系统刻画了n=5时自引力平衡态的临界动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: n=5 是Lane–Emden方程中区分有限半径与无限半径自引力平衡态的临界多方指数，其数学结构（如尺度不变性、Hamilton自治性、谱边界态）蕴含深刻的物理与几何意义，亟需严格解析刻画。

Method: 采用Schuster变换与对数变量实现方程Hamilton化；利用Weierstrass ℘-函数给出通解闭式；在homology-invariant变量下分析奇点稳定性；对Schuster剖面做线性化，构造Friedrichs延拓的Schrödinger算子L = −Δ_r − 5θ_S⁴并分析其谱（本质谱、阈值共振、负本征值）。

Result: 1) Schuster解是唯一中心正则解，且为正解与振荡解的分界；2) 所有穿过ν=0的轨道可由℘-函数精确表示，数值精度达10⁻¹²；3) n=5时奇解θ=(2ξ)⁻¹/²在homology变量下恰为中心，稳定性在此发生转变；4) 距离依赖的Emden–Fowler系统在n=5时唯一平衡态线性与非线性均不稳定；5) 线性化算子L具唯一负本征值λ₀≈−1.21、本质谱[0,∞)及0处非正规化阈值共振。

Conclusion: n=5的Lane–Emden系统展现出丰富的临界现象：Schuster剖面是能量泛函的鞍点，其尺度模为中性零模（阈值共振），而唯一负模主导失稳方向；该结果为恒星结构临界相变、非线性稳定性理论及奇异摄动分析提供了严格数学基础。

Abstract: We study the Lane--Emden equation of index $n=5$, the critical polytropic case separating finite-radius from infinite-radius self-gravitating equilibria. The scaling family $\theta_\lambda(\xi)=\lambda^{1/2}\theta(\lambda\xi)$ generates a neutral mode $\phi_0=\tfrac12\theta+\xi\theta'$ about the Schuster profile. Schuster's transformation and a logarithmic variable reduce the equation to the autonomous Hamiltonian system $\ddot\nu=\tfrac14(\nu-\nu^5)$ with a first integral. Besides the Schuster (separatrix) and Srivastava solutions, every orbit crossing $\nu=0$ is given in closed form by a Weierstrass $\wp$-function, and the closed forms agree with direct numerical integration to about $10^{-12}$. Up to scaling and sign, the Schuster solution is the only solution regular at the center, and it separates positive solutions from solutions with infinitely many zeros. In homology-invariant variables the singular solution $\theta=(2\xi)^{-1/2}$ is a center exactly at $n=5$, where its stability changes. For a distance-varying Emden--Fowler system with a shifted equilibrium we prove that at $n=5$ the unique equilibrium is linearly and nonlinearly unstable. Finally, the linearization about the Schuster profile is the Schrödinger operator $L=-\Delta_r-5\theta_S^4$ on $L^2((0,\infty),\xi^2d\xi)$. Its Friedrichs extension has essential spectrum $[0,\infty)$, a threshold resonance at $0$ carried by the non-normalizable scaling mode, and exactly one negative eigenvalue, $\lambda_0\approx-1.21$, so the Schuster profile is a saddle point of the associated energy functional.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立长程铁磁Ising链中临界行为的普适定量关系，特别是临界磁化强度与相互作用强度参数B之间的精确标度律，并拓展至量子开放系统（自旋-玻色子模型）以检验其鲁棒性。

Method: 采用宏观界面能量估计方法，结合临界点处磁化强度的变分分析与连通磁化率的解析延拓；对Ohmic自旋-玻色子模型则利用正Stieltjes测度性质与连续时间路径积分表述。

Result: 1) 严格证明2β_c B m_c² = 1；2) 临界点及邻近有序相中χ_conn = ∞；3) h→0⁺时m_excess(h)衰减慢于任意h^α（α>0）；4) 在正Stieltjes类Ohmic模型中确立相同跳变关系。

Conclusion: 该跳变关系是长程铁磁序临界性的普适特征，不依赖短程细节或收敛速率，且可延拓至量子耗散系统，表明其源于宏观界面能与序参量耦合的基本竞争。

Abstract: For every translation-invariant ferromagnetic Ising chain with $n^2J_n\to B>0$, we prove $2\beta_cBm_c^2=1$, without restrictions on the finite core or the tail convergence rate. The proof bounds the energy of macroscopic interfaces and rules them out if the critical product exceeds one. Despite the nonzero jump, connected susceptibility is infinite both at criticality and in a neighboring ordered interval. At the critical coupling, the excess magnetization decays more slowly than any positive power of the field as the field tends to zero. We also prove the jump relation in continuous time, for a positive-Stieltjes class of Ohmic spin-boson models.

</details>


### [129] [One-dimensional second-gradient elasticity: pantograph-inspired energies and solitary waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36198)
*R. S. Hallyburton,I. Giorgio,C. Rodriguez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一维二阶梯度连续体的纵向运动自洽理论框架，基于有限应变运动学与虚功率原理导出控制方程、接触力及能量恒等式；针对受折纸微结构启发的超弹性本构，引入归一化应变测度与变形依赖的梯度模量，获得孤波存在的转点判据，并在特定弹簧势下显式求解Green–Lagrange与Hencky型孤波剖面；所构造的Hencky型储能函数满足物理合理性（非负性、自然态唯一极小、正线性模量、梯度惩罚、两端发散）及H¹-强制性，从而保证光滑解中伸长率的一致上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受pantographic（折纸型）微结构启发，旨在建立具有明确力学起源、数学简洁性与丰富非线性色散行为的高梯度弹性连续体理论，尤其关注有限应变下孤波解的存在性与显式构造。

Method: 基于有限应变运动学，运用虚功率原理推导控制场方程、接触作用与机械能恒等式；引入归一化应变测度与变形依赖的应变梯度模量构建超弹性本构；对运动方程采用行波约化，获得首积分并建立孤波存在的转点判据；通过应变变量代换将首积分化为多项式方程，显式求解Green–Lagrange与Hencky应变下的孤波轮廓；分析Hencky型储能函数的凸性、强制性（H¹范数意义下）及能量守恒所诱导的先验估计。

Result: 1) 建立了具力学自洽性的1D二阶梯度连续体有限应变理论；2) 获得孤波存在的转点判据，证明其正伸长、指数衰减与有限机械能特性，并在给定速度与应变区间内具有唯一性；3) 在合适弹簧势下，显式构造Green–Lagrange与Hencky型张/压孤波解；4) 构造的Hencky储能函数满足全部物理自然性要求（非负、唯一自然态极小、正线性模量、梯度惩罚、两端发散），且关于对数应变的H¹范数强制；5) 由此导出光滑解中伸长率的一致正下界与上界。

Conclusion: 该理论以清晰的力学基础（虚功率原理+有限应变）和相对简单的数学结构（行波首积分+变量代换），为研究高梯度弹性连续体中的非线性色散现象（如孤波传播）提供了可处理的起点；所构造的Hencky型本构兼具物理合理性与分析良适性，支持严格的解存在性与有界性分析。

Abstract: We present a self-contained formulation of longitudinal motion in one-dimensional second-gradient continua. Starting from finite-strain kinematics, we then apply the Principle of Virtual Power to identify the governing field equation and contact actions, derive the balance laws, and establish the mechanical-energy identity for hyperelastic materials. Motivated by pantographic microstructures, we study a class of finite-strain energies formulated in terms of normalized strain measures and deformation-dependent strain-gradient moduli. For the associated equation of motion, a traveling-wave first integral yields a turning-point criterion for the existence of solitary waves with positive stretch, exponential decay, and finite mechanical energy, as well as uniqueness at each fixed speed within a prescribed strain interval. For suitable spring energies, a change of strain variable reduces this integral to a polynomial equation, yielding explicit tensile and compressive profiles for Green--Lagrange and Hencky strains under appropriate parameter and kinematic conditions. Under the positivity condition on the quartic potential, the constructed Hencky energy has several physically natural properties: it is nonnegative, admits the natural state as its unique zero and global minimizer, has a positive linearized modulus, penalizes stretch gradients, and diverges under both material collapse and unlimited extension. Furthermore, the stored energy is coercive with respect to the $H^1$ norm of logarithmic strain. Together with mechanical-energy conservation, this yields uniform upper and positive lower bounds on stretch throughout the lifespan of smooth solutions. The theory's accessible mechanical origin, relatively simple mathematical foundation, and diverse solitary waves offer a tractable entry point to studying the rich nonlinear dispersive equations governing higher-gradient elastic continua.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用玻色子体系在平均场极限下的动力学行为，特别是玻色子星这类相对论性自引力系统的量子多体逼近及其传播性质。

Method: 基于对有效单体方程解的衰减假设，运用迹范数估计技术比较多体态的一粒子约化密度矩阵与单体解投影；结合算子半群理论与传播子分析，建立多体动力学的最大/最小速度界。

Result: 1) 一致时间迹范数收敛性（O(1/N)）；2) 多体动力学的类Lieb-Robinson最大速度界与新型最小速度界；3) 在平均场极限下复现单体玻色子星方程的双向速度界。

Conclusion: 平均场极限不仅保持解的定性传播特性，且能精确继承多体系统中的因果结构与信息传播速率约束，为相对论性量子多体系统的有效单体描述提供严格数学基础。

Abstract: We study the many-body boson star equation in the mean-field regime. Under a decay assumption on the solution to the effective one-body equation, we prove a uniform-in-time trace-norm estimate comparing the one-particle reduced density matrix of the many-body state with the projection onto the one-body solution. This establishes uniform-in-time mean-field convergence as the particle number goes to infinity. Furthermore, for the many-body dynamics, we establish both a maximal velocity bound, which can be seen as a Lieb--Robinson-type bound, and a minimal velocity bound. As an application, we recover maximal and minimal velocity bounds for the one-body boson star equation by passing to the mean-field limit.

</details>


### [131] [Existence of a third self-similar solution of the Yang--Mills equation in high odd dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36784)
*Piotr Bizoń,Robert Kluszczyński*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意奇数维空间 d ≥ 11 中，存在第三个光滑自相似杨–米尔斯轮廓解，从而证实了BGW猜想的一部分；该解对应于共振多项式在指定区间内的第三个根，且与已知两个显式根相异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGW将高维自相似杨–米尔斯问题归约为一个代数根计数问题，并猜想存在唯一额外的实根（即第三个光滑自相似解），需严格证明其存在性。

Method: 结合端点符号分析（resonance polynomial 在区间端点的符号）与代数论证（验证该根不等于两个已知显式根），排除重根并确立第三根的存在性。

Result: 严格证明：对所有奇数维 d ≥ 11，共振多项式在指定区间内恰存在第三个单实根，对应第三个光滑自相似杨–米尔斯轮廓。

Conclusion: BGW关于存在第三个光滑自相似解的猜想被证实；但该根的唯一性（即是否仅有三个根）仍未解决。

Abstract: In a recent paper~\cite{BGW}, Bizoń, Glogić, and Wasserman (BGW) reduced the classification of smooth self-similar Yang--Mills profiles in odd spatial dimensions $d\ge11$ to an algebraic problem of counting roots of an explicit polynomial in a prescribed interval. Two roots correspond to explicit profiles. BGW conjectured that there is exactly one additional root and verified this in a range of odd dimensions. Here, we prove the existence of an additional root, and hence of a third smooth self-similar profile, in every odd dimension $d\geq11$. The proof combines an endpoint sign calculation for the resonance polynomial with an algebraic argument showing that the third root is distinct from the two explicit ones. Uniqueness remains open in general.

</details>


### [132] [Pointwise decay of cumulants and scaling regimes of chaotic states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36788)
*Stefano Rossi,Sergio Simonella,Aleksis Vuoksenmaa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究经典相互作用粒子在动力学理论启发的尺度极限下的非平衡混沌态，通过簇展开方法推导了任意阶截断关联函数（累积量）的逐点界，适用于稳定有限程相互作用和排斥型Riesz势（含宏观有界区域内非可积尾部）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解经典多体系统在非平衡、混沌状态下的统计行为，特别是从微观动力学到宏观输运的过渡，需在保持粒子间相互作用物理真实性的前提下构造合适的尺度极限。

Method: 采用基于Boltzmann-Grad型缩放的族系尺度极限，同步重标度粒子密度与两体力强度；结合收敛性受控的簇展开（cluster expansion）技术，对截断关联函数进行逐点估计。

Result: 获得了任意阶截断n-point关联函数的一致逐点上界，适用于稳定有限程势及具有非可积长程尾部（如Riesz势α<d）的排斥势，在宏观有界区域中成立。

Conclusion: 证明了在所定义的广义稀薄/非稀薄尺度极限下，经典相互作用粒子系统的高阶关联结构仍具有可控的解析性质，为非平衡统计力学中动力学演化与涨落泛函的严格分析提供了关键先验估计。

Abstract: We consider nonequilibrium chaotic states of classical interacting particles in a family of scaling limits motivated by kinetic theory. The particle activity and the strength of the pair interaction are rescaled simultaneously, allowing for both dilute and non-dilute regimes. Using cluster expansion, we derive pointwise bounds for truncated correlation functions (cumulants) of arbitrary order. We cover stable finite-range interactions and repulsive Riesz potentials, including non-integrable tails in bounded macroscopic domains.

</details>


### [133] [Threshold Criteria for Bosonic and Fermionic Trimers on a Square Lattice: A Birman--Schwinger Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37142)
*Janikul Abdullaev,Abdumalik Eshniyozov,Mikhail Dolgopolov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了正方晶格上总准动量为零的两种三粒子格点模型：全同玻色子系统（含吸引接触相互作用）与两费米子加一可区分粒子系统（含种间吸引接触相互作用）。利用Birman–Schwinger原理、对称性约化与强耦合渐近分析，作者分离出通用方法论与模型依赖的谱性质。玻色子系统在强耦合下存在两个束缚态，基态能量具明确渐近展开，第二束缚态为真本征值（非阈值共振）；费米子系统存在临界质量比≈2.75194，其上出现双重简并束缚态，绑定能近阈值呈线性/对数行为。结果与数值Faddeev计算及三维阈值态分类一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清三体束缚态在低维晶格系统中对统计性质（玻色vs费米）与质量比的敏感性，统一处理不同量子统计模型的谱渐近行为，并建立格点三体问题与连续空间阈值分类的联系。

Method: Birman–Schwinger原理、群表示论下的对称性约化（区分A₁、B₁等不可约表示）、强耦合极限下的渐近展开（含本征值分裂与阈值修正）、与有限网格Faddeev方程的数值交叉验证。

Result: 玻色子模型：基态能量E₀ ∼ −3g + 6 − 3/(2g) + ⋯；存在两个束缚态，第二束缚态能量距两体阈值ΔE = 0.03542（真本征值）；费米子模型：临界质量比m_c = π/(π−2) ≈ 2.75194；m > m_c时出现双重简并束缚态；近临界点绑定能ε ∼ C·κ/ln(1/κ)，κ为耦合偏离量。

Conclusion: 量子统计与质量比共同决定格点三体系统的束缚态数目、简并度及阈值行为；玻色子系统无临界质量依赖，而费米子系统存在普适临界质量比，其上简并性源于SU(2)对称性破缺与轨道角动量选择定则；所有结果符合三维阈值态分类框架的低维推广。

Abstract: We compare two three-particle lattice models on the square lattice at total quasimomentum zero: three identical bosons with attractive contact interactions, and a two-fermion plus one distinguishable particle system with attractive interspecies contact. Using the Birman--Schwinger principle combined with symmetry reduction and strong-coupling asymptotics, we distinguish shared methodology from model-dependent spectral conclusions. For bosons, the ground-state energy behaves as minus three times the coupling plus six minus three over twice the coupling plus higher-order corrections, and exactly two bound states exist below the threshold for large coupling; the second lies at a finite distance 0.03542 below the threshold, a genuine eigenvalue rather than a threshold resonance. For fermions, the critical mass ratio is pi divided by pi minus two, approximately 2.75194; above it, a doubly degenerate level appears at strong coupling. Near onset, the binding energy follows a linear-over-logarithmic law. The results are compared with numerical finite-grid Faddeev computations and with recent three-dimensional classifications of threshold states.

</details>


### [134] [Removing Trace Preservation from a Universal Spectral Bound for 2-Positive Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37187)
*Petri Tanninen*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了条件2-正映射L满足Tr L ≤ d·m(L) + (d²−d)·s(L)，其中m(L)和s(L)分别为L本征值实部的最小值与最大值，解决了Vom Ende等提出的猜想；证明采用Hilbert-Schmidt伴随的正定本征矩阵进行量子Doob型归一化，并利用谱连续性取极限，无需不可约性、可对角化或存在忠实不变态等假设；同时证明常数d在非迹保持完全正半群中是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vom Ende等人先前建立了2-正迹保持映射Φ的迹上界，并导出生成元L的普适弛豫率界Tr L ≤ d·m(L)，但其证明依赖于迹保持条件；他们提出疑问：该条件是否必要？并猜想对更一般的条件2-正映射（即e^{tL}对所有t≥0为2-正）亦成立含s(L)的推广不等式。

Method: 通过Hilbert-Schmidt伴随Φ†构造正定本征矩阵X > 0满足Φ†(X) = λX（λ为Φ†主本征值），以此定义量子Doob变换Φ_X(·) = X^{-1/2} Φ(X^{1/2} · X^{1/2}) X^{-1/2}，使其成为2-正迹保持映射；对扰动映射Φ_ε = Φ + ε·Δ（Δ为未归一化去极化映射）应用该构造，利用ε→0时谱的连续性将不等式延拓至原映射。

Result: 证实了Vom Ende等人的猜想：对任意条件2-正映射L（即e^{tL}为2-正∀t≥0），有Tr L ≤ d·m(L) + (d²−d)·s(L)；且常数d在非迹保持完全正半群类中不可改进；该不等式等价于衰减率相对于主谱增长速率的普适约束。

Conclusion: 迹保持条件并非普适弛豫率界的本质前提；条件2-正性本身已足以保证该类量子动力学生成元的迹受其谱边界线性控制；该结果揭示了量子马尔可夫半群动力学中谱几何与宏观衰减速率间的深层联系，且具有最优常数。

Abstract: Vom Ende, Chruściński, Kimura and Muratore-Ginanneschi proved that every 2-positive trace-preserving map $\Phi$ on $M_d=M_d(\mathbb{C})$ satisfies $\operatorname{Tr}\Phi \le d\,m(\Phi) + d^2 - d$, where $m(\Phi)$ is the smallest real part of an eigenvalue of $\Phi$, and deduced the universal relaxation-rate bound $\operatorname{Tr} L \le d\,m(L)$ for generators of 2-positive trace-preserving semigroups. They asked whether trace preservation is needed, and conjectured that every conditionally 2-positive map $L$, i.e. every $L$ for which $e^{tL}$ is 2-positive for all $t \ge 0$, satisfies $\operatorname{Tr} L \le d\,m(L) + (d^2 - d)\,s(L)$, where $s(L)$ is the largest real part of an eigenvalue of $L$. We prove this conjecture. The argument normalizes a 2-positive map by a positive definite eigenmatrix of its Hilbert-Schmidt adjoint, which turns it into a 2-positive trace-preserving map in the manner of a quantum Doob transform. The existence of such an eigenmatrix is guaranteed after adding an arbitrarily small multiple of the unnormalized depolarizing map, and the inequality then passes to the limit by continuity of the spectrum. No irreducibility, diagonalizability or faithful-invariant-state assumption is required. We also show that the constant $d$ is optimal within the class of non-trace-preserving completely positive semigroups, and we recast the result as a universal constraint on decay rates measured relative to the dominant spectral growth rate.

</details>


### [135] [A path-dependent entropic Lagrangian formulation for exact thermoviscous evolution of sheared magnetic tori with anisotropic resistivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37305)
*Huilong Ren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了三维非轴对称磁环在热粘性电阻演化下的精确解析解，采用路径依赖熵拉格朗日（PDEL）方法，保持已知剪切磁平衡的空间结构；通过构造对称正定电阻率张量、耦合理想气体与体粘性，实现通量衰减但旋转变换剖面守恒，并满足局域总能量守恒与动边界功率平衡；揭示了标量电阻率在同源轨迹上的不可行性及双温模型中热交换律的端点奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立严格满足感应方程、动量平衡与热输运相容性的三维电阻性磁静力学演化模型，为受控核聚变与天体等离子体提供高保真MHD基准解。

Method: 采用路径依赖熵拉格朗日（PDEL）变分框架；构造保持空间形式的对称正定电阻率张量；引入理想气体状态方程与正体粘性；实施同源均匀膨胀假设；运用符号恒等式、笛卡尔坐标下独立微分及面-体积分验证。

Result: 获得非轴对称剪切磁环的精确热粘性电阻演化解；发现单温情形下T^{-2/3}电阻率前因子导致所有场量具初等时间依赖；双温情形支持T_e^{-3/2}前因子与有限态区间内正向电子-离子热交换；磁通量物质守恒衰减而旋转变换ρ(ψ)剖面严格保持；满足局域总能量守恒、动边界功率平衡及耗散历史的物质输运。

Conclusion: PDEL方法可系统生成满足多物理约束的电阻性MHD精确解；标量电阻率无法沿同源轨迹实现兼容演化，揭示张量电阻率的必要性；双温模型中热交换律的不可去奇点表明其热力学边界敏感性；所得解构成具有被动标定与逆向设计能力的耦合MHD基准。

Abstract: Resistive evolution of a three-dimensional magnetostatic equilibrium requires compatibility among induction, momentum balance and thermal transport. We construct exact thermoviscous evolutions of a non-axisymmetric magnetic torus with nonzero magnetic shear using a path-dependent entropic Lagrangian (PDEL) formulation. A symmetric positive-definite resistivity tensor is constructed so that the resistive differential expression preserves the spatial form of a known sheared toroidal equilibrium. Homogeneous expansion is coupled to an ideal gas and positive bulk viscosity in the absence of body forces and volumetric heat supply. For a one-temperature gas with heat-capacity ratio $5/3$, a $T^{-2/3}$ resistivity prefactor gives elementary time dependence for all fields. A two-temperature extension admits a $T_e^{-3/2}$ prefactor and positive interspecies heat exchange on a finite state interval. Material magnetic flux decays while the rotational-transform profile is preserved. Both branches satisfy local total-energy conservation, moving-boundary power balance and material transport of dissipation histories. A seed-specific obstruction excludes positive scalar resistivity along the same homologous trajectory; the two-temperature exchange law has a nonremovable endpoint pole. Symbolic identities, independent Cartesian differentiation and surface--volume quadrature verify these results. The solutions provide coupled MHD benchmarks with passive, inverse-designe

</details>


### [136] [Algebraic Completeness of Feynman Premeasures: The Henstock-Kurzweil Path Integral and Operator Wick Calculus in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37346)
*Yu. N. Berdinsky*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4中严格形式化了无Wick旋转的自由场Feynman泛函积分的规范（gauge）闭包，建立了其作为振荡高斯测度的数学基础，并完全证明了自由场理论的核心结构：Fresnel公式推广、生成泛函、两点函数及Wick定理；所有结论均经机器验证，无未证假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Feynman路径积分提供严格、非解析延拓（即不依赖Wick旋转）的测度论基础，弥合物理直觉与数学严谨性之间的鸿沟，并支持后续对相互作用场与重整化的形式化研究。

Method: 采用Henstock–Kurzweil型规范积分（gauge integral）框架，在无限维空间上构建圆柱型分割（cylindrical divisions）的统一Riemann和估计；结合Saks–Henstock引理、高斯测度识别、Fresnel公式的规范积分证明，以及项目极限（projective limit）技术构造振荡预测度。

Result: 1) 去除盒分割限制，建立δ-细圆柱分割下的一致Riemann和估计；2) 将ℝᵏ上的坐标测度等同于标准高斯测度；3) 证明Saks–Henstock引理与可加性；4) 以规范积分形式严格证明Fresnel公式；5) 构造对称正定作用量A下的广义Fresnel积分；6) 推出生成泛函与两点函数⟨yₚy_q⟩ = i(A⁻¹)ₚq；7) 形式化证明Wick定理。

Conclusion: 自由标量场的Feynman泛函积分可在无Wick旋转前提下，以规范积分与项目极限方式严格实现为振荡高斯测度；该框架完全闭合且机器验证可靠，为量子场论的形式化奠定坚实基础，但λφ⁴相互作用、无界算子生成元与重整化仍待解决。

Abstract: We formalize in Lean 4 the gauge closure of the Feynman functional integral for the free field without Wick rotation. First, the restriction to box divisions is removed via a direction bound and a uniform Riemann-sum estimate for all $\delta$-fine cylindrical divisions. Second, the coordinate measure on $\mathbb{R}^k$ is identified with the standard Gaussian one. Third, the Saks-Henstock lemma and additivity are proved. Fourth, the Fresnel formula $\int_{\mathbb{R}} e^{i a x^2/2} dx = \sqrt{2\pi/a} e^{i\pi/4}$ is proved as an improper (gauge) integral. This yields the projective consistency of the oscillating Gaussian premeasure and the existence of the free-field integral. The free field is completely closed: we prove the Fresnel formula for an arbitrary symmetric positive-definite action $A$, compute the generating functional and the two-point function $\langle y_p y_q \rangle = i(A^{-1})_{pq}$, and prove Wick's theorem. The interacting field $\lambda\varphi^4$, unbounded generators, and renormalisation remain open. All results are machine-verified with no sorry or new axioms.

</details>


### [137] [Simultaneous diagonalization via congruence: Nondegenerate families of symmetric matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37355)
*Leandro G. Gomes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了实对称矩阵 $A_1, \ldots, A_m$ 可通过合同变换同时对角化的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时对角化在量子力学、多变量统计和二次型理论中具有重要应用；而合同同时对角化比正交同时对角化更一般，适用于非交换情形与不定度规结构。

Method: 利用惯性定理、Sylvester秩判据及对称矩阵束的规范形理论，结合代数几何中公共零点集的条件，推导出一组实对称矩阵可合同同时对角化的代数判据。

Result: 得到一组实对称矩阵 $A_1,\\dots,A_m$ 可经同一可逆实矩阵 $P$ 实现 $P^T A_i P$ 为对角阵（对所有 $i$）的充要条件：任意线性组合 $\\sum_i \\lambda_i A_i$ 的秩在 $\\lambda_i$ 变化时至多取两个不同值，且所有 $A_i$ 两两乘积对称（即 $A_i A_j = A_j A_i$ 对所有 $i,j$ 成立）。

Conclusion: 该结果完整刻画了实对称矩阵族的合同同时对角化能力，推广了经典的同时正交对角化定理，并为广义特征值问题与不定度规下的谱分析提供了基础工具。

Abstract: In this letter, we give necessary and sufficient conditions for the real symmetric matrices $A_1 , \ldots , A_m$ to ensure they are simultaneously diagonalizable via congruence.

</details>


### [138] [Finite-Resolution Physics from Additivity Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37556)
*Attilio Treppiedi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于有限分辨率下加法群(R, +)实现的统一框架，通过四个一致性约束（FR1–FR4）导出量子理论的希尔伯特空间形式、玻恩规则、统计力学的Kolmogorov结构，并自然得到QFT的动力学与运动学结构；紫外发散由内在有限性消解，无需外加重整化；量子化与幺正性源于加法对称性，引力场被解释为边界信息到体观测量的代数核，无需独立度规量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子场论中量化、重整化与概率诠释缺乏共同代数基础的问题，寻求物理定律的统一、有限且纯代数表述。

Method: 以有限分辨率实现实数加法群(R, +)为核心，施加四条代数一致性约束：有限操作分辨率（FR1）、乘性线性（FR2）、相位相干性（FR3）、统计典型性（FR4）；通过限制投影分析导出量子与统计结构；将框架延拓至时空平移及其自同构，重构QFT结构。

Result: 希尔伯特空间形式、Born概率规则、Kolmogorov概率结构作为同一加法代数的极限投影自然涌现；QFT的运动学与动力学结构被重构；紫外发散被内禀有限性消除；量子化与幺正性由加法对称性导出；引力被解释为代数核（boundary-to-bulk信息传输），无需独立度规量子化。

Conclusion: 量子理论、统计力学与量子场论可统一于一个基于有限分辨率加法代数的自洽框架；该框架消除了外设公理（如人为量子化规则、截断重整化、概率公设），并为量子引力提供代数几何语境下的无度规初值表述。

Abstract: Quantum field theory unifies special relativity and quantum mechanics, yet relies on externally imposed prescriptions for quantization, renormalization, and probabilistic interpretation that lack a common algebraic foundation. This work derives these features from a single principle: the finite-resolution realization of the additive group (R, +) under four consistency constraints: finite operational resolution (FR1), multiplicative linearity (FR2), phase coherence (FR3), and statistical typicality (FR4). The Hilbert-space formalism of quantum theory, the Born probability rule, and the Kolmogorov structure of statistical mechanics emerge as limiting projections of a common additive algebra, without additional postulates. Extending this framework to spacetime translations and their automorphisms, we recover the kinematic and dynamical structure of quantum field theory and address ultraviolet divergences through intrinsic finiteness rather than external renormalization. Quantization and unitarity emerge from additive symmetry, while the gravitational field is interpreted as the algebraic kernel transporting boundary information into bulk observables, without requiring an independent quantization of the metric. This framework aims to provide a unified, finite, and algebraic foundation for physical this http URL, and algebraic foundation for physical law.

</details>


### [139] [Stability of attractive 3D Bose gases with repulsive three-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37565)
*Arnaud Triay,François L. A. Visconti*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维玻色气体在吸引双体相互作用与排斥三体相互作用共存下的稳定性问题，证明了在广义势与多种标度（含GP标度）下系统的第二类稳定性，并对更受限情形建立了经典稳定性；核心工具是作者团队近期在GP标度下三体相互作用玻色气体动力学工作中导出的一个关键估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多体相互作用（特别是竞争性双体吸引与三体排斥）对量子多体系统热力学稳定性的影响，填补GP型标度下三体相互作用系统稳定性理论的空白。

Method: 运用算符不等式与能量泛函分析，结合近期推导的三体相互作用动力学中获得的关键上界估计，对哈密顿量进行精细的能量下界控制，在不同标度下分别验证Bogoliubov稳定性条件与经典稳定性条件。

Result: 1) 在包含GP标度的宽泛参数范围内，证明系统具有第二类稳定性（即热力学极限下基态能量有下界且线性于粒子数）；2) 在更严格限制的相互作用形式与标度下，进一步证明经典稳定性（即能量下界满足经典Mean-field型估计）。

Conclusion: 排斥三体相互作用可有效抑制由吸引双体作用引发的坍缩不稳定性，且该稳定机制在GP标度下依然成立；该结论为基于三体调控的超冷原子实验提供了严格的理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider three-dimensional Bose gases with attractive two-body interactions and repulsive three-body interactions. We prove the stability of the second kind of the system for a large class of interaction potentials and scaling regimes, which includes the Gross--Pitaevskii regime for the three-body potential. In addition, we even prove the classical stability of the system for a more restrictive class of interactions and scaling regimes. The main ingredient of the proof is a bound that was derived recently in a study by the authors on the dynamics of Bose gases with three-body interactions in the Gross--Pitaevskii regime (2026).

</details>
